




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


suttanipātapāḷi


1. uragavaggau

1. uragasuttaṃ

1.

 yau  yau vai (syā॰) uppatitaṃ vinaiti kaudhaṃ, visaṭaṃ sappavisaṃva ausadhaihi ausadhaibhi (ka॰)|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ jiṇṇamiva tacaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), jiṇṇamivā tacaṃ (?) purāṇaṃ||

2.

 yau rāgamudacchidā asaisaṃ, bhisapupphaṃva sarauruhaṃ sarairuhaṃ (ka॰) vigayha|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ, purāṇaṃ||

3.

 yau  taṇhamudacchidā asaisaṃ, saritaṃ sīghasaraṃ visausayitvā|

sau  bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

4.

 yau  mānamudabbadhī asaisaṃ, naḷasaituṃva sudubbalaṃ mahaughau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

5.

 yau nājjhagamā bhavaisu sāraṃ, vicinaṃ pupphamiva pupphamiva (bahūsu) udumbaraisu|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

6.

 yassantaratau na santi kaupā, itibhavābhavatañ‍ca itibbhavābhavatañ‍ca (ka॰) vītivattau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

7.

 yassa  vitak‍kā vidhūpitā, ajjhattaṃ suvikappitā asaisā|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

8.

 yau nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ ac‍cagamā imaṃ papañ‍caṃ|

sau  bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

9.

 yau nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ vitathamidanti ñatvā utvā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) laukai|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

10.

 yau nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ vitathamidanti vītalaubhau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

11.

 yau  nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ vitathamidanti vītarāgau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

12.

 yau nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ vitathamidanti vītadausau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

13.

 yau  nāc‍casārī na pac‍casārī, sabbaṃ vitathamidanti vītamauhau|

sau  bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

14.

 yassānusayā na santi kaici, mūlā ca akusalā samūhatāsai|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

15.

 yassa darathajā na santi kaici, auraṃ āgamanāya pac‍cayāsai|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

16.

 yassa vanathajā na santi kaici, vinibandhāya bhavāya haitukappā|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

17.

 yau  nīvaraṇai pahāya pañ‍ca, anighau tiṇṇakathaṃkathau visal‍lau|

sau bhikkhu jahāti aurapāraṃ, uragau jiṇṇamivattacaṃ purāṇaṃ||

uragasuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dhaniyasuttaṃ

18.

 ‘‘pak‍kaudanau  duddhakhīrauhamasmi, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

anutīrai mahiyā samānavāsau|

chan‍nā kuṭi āhitau gini, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

19.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanau vigatakhilauhamasmi vigatakhīlauhamasmi (sī॰ pī॰), (iti bhagavā)

anutīrai mahiyaikarattivāsau|

vivaṭā kuṭi nibbutau gini, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

20.

 ‘‘andhakamakasā  na vij‍jarai, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

kacchai rūḷhatiṇai caranti gāvau|

vuṭṭhimpi sahaiyyumāgataṃ, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

21.

 ‘‘baddhāsi bhisī susaṅkhatā, (iti bhagavā)

tiṇṇau pāragatau vinaiyya aughaṃ|

atthau bhisiyā na vij‍jati, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

22.

 ‘‘gaupī  mama assavā alaulā, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

dīgharattaṃ dīgharatta (ka॰) saṃvāsiyā manāpā|

tassā  na suṇāmi kiñ‍ci pāpaṃ, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

23.

 ‘‘cittaṃ mama assavaṃ vimuttaṃ, (iti bhagavā)

dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitaṃ sudantaṃ|

pāpaṃ pana mai na vij‍jati, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

24.

 ‘‘attavaitanabhatauhamasmi, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

puttā ca mai samāniyā araugā|

taisaṃ na suṇāmi kiñ‍ci pāpaṃ, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

25.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ bhatakausmi kassaci, (iti bhagavā)

nibbiṭṭhaina carāmi sabbalaukai|

atthau bhatiyā na vij‍jati, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

26.

 ‘‘atthi vasā atthi dhainupā, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

gaudharaṇiyau pavaiṇiyaupi atthi|

usabhaupi gavampatīdha atthi, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

27.

 ‘‘natthi  vasā natthi dhainupā, (iti bhagavā)

gaudharaṇiyau pavaiṇiyaupi natthi|

usabhaupi  gavampatīdha natthi, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

28.

 ‘‘khilā nikhātā asampavaidhī, (iti dhaniyau gaupau)

dāmā muñ‍jamayā navā susaṇṭhānā|

na hi sakkhinti dhainupāpi chaittuṃ chaituṃ (ka॰), atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

29.

 ‘‘usabhauriva chaitva chaitvā (syā॰ ka॰) bandhanāni, (iti bhagavā)

nāgau pūtilataṃva dālayitvā pūtilataṃ padālayitvā (syā॰ ka॰)|

nāhaṃ punupaissaṃ puna upaissaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), punupaiyya (ka॰) gabbhasaiyyaṃ, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva’’||

30.

 ‘‘nin‍nañ‍ca thalañ‍ca pūrayantau, mahāmaighau pavassi tāvadaiva|

sutvā daivassa vassatau, imamatthaṃ dhaniyau abhāsatha||

31.

 ‘‘lābhā  vata nau anappakā, yai mayaṃ bhagavantaṃ addasāma|

saraṇaṃ taṃ upaima cakkhuma, satthā nau hauhi tuvaṃ mahāmuni||

32.

 ‘‘gaupī ca ahañ‍ca assavā, brahmacariyaṃ brahmacariya (ka॰) sugatai carāmasai|

jātimaraṇassa  pāragū pāragā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), dukkhassantakarā bhavāmasai’’||

33.

 ‘‘nandati  puttaihi puttimā, (iti mārau pāpimā)

gaumā gaumikau (sī॰ pī॰), gaupikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), gaupiyau (ka॰) gauhi tathaiva nandati|

upadhī hi narassa nandanā, na hi sau nandati yau nirūpadhi’’||

34.

 ‘‘saucati puttaihi puttimā, (iti bhagavā)

gaupiyau gauhi tathaiva saucati|

upadhī hi narassa saucanā, na hi sau saucati yau nirūpadhī’’ti||

dhaniyasuttaṃ dutiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. khaggavisāṇasuttaṃ

35.

 sabbaisu  bhūtaisu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ, avihaiṭhayaṃ añ‍ñatarampi taisaṃ|

na puttamicchaiyya kutau sahāyaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

36.

 saṃsaggajātassa  bhavanti snaihā, snaihanvayaṃ  dukkhamidaṃ pahauti|

ādīnavaṃ snaihajaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

37.

 mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau, hāpaiti atthaṃ paṭibaddhacittau|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai sandhavai (ka॰) paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

38.

 vaṃsau visālauva yathā visattau, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā|

vaṃsak‍kaḷīrauva  vaṃsakaḷīrauva (sī॰), vaṃsākaḷīrauva (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), vaṃsaikaḷīrauva (niddaisa) asajjamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

39.

 migau arañ‍ñamhi yathā abaddhau abandhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yainicchakaṃ gacchati gaucarāya|

viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

40.

 āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāya|

anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

41.

 khiḍḍā  ratī hauti sahāyamajjhai, puttaisu ca vipulaṃ hauti paimaṃ|

piyavippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

42.

 cātuddisau  appaṭighau ca hauti, santussamānau itarītaraina|

parissayānaṃ sahitā achambhī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

43.

 dussaṅgahā pabbajitāpi aikai, athau gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā|

appaussuk‍kau paraputtaisu hutvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

44.

 auraupayitvā  gihibyañ‍janāni gihivyañ‍janāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sañchin‍napattau saṃsīnapattau (sī॰) yathā kauviḷārau|

chaitvāna  vīrau gihibandhanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

45.

 sacai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāridhīraṃ|

abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni, caraiyya tainattamanau satīmā||

46.

 nau  cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāridhīraṃ|

rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya, aikau carai mātaṅgarañ‍ñaiva nāgau||

47.

 addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadaṃ, saiṭṭhā samā saivitabbā sahāyā|

aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

48.

 disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni, kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitāni|

saṅghaṭṭamānāni duvai bhujasmiṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

49.

 aivaṃ  dutiyaina dutiyaina (sabbattha) sahā mamassa, vācābhilāpau abhisaj‍janā vā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

50.

 kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramā, virūparūpaina mathainti cittaṃ|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

51.

 ītī  ca gaṇḍau ca upaddavau ca, raugau ca sal‍lañ‍ca bhayañ‍ca maitaṃ|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

52.

 sītañ‍ca  uṇhañ‍ca khudaṃ pipāsaṃ, vātātapai ḍaṃsasarīsapai ḍaṃsasiriṃsapai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca|

sabbānipaitāni abhisambhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

53.

 nāgauva yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvā, sañ‍jātakhandhau padumī uḷārau|

yathābhirantaṃ viharaṃ viharai (sī॰ pī॰ niddaisa) arañ‍ñai, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

54.

 aṭṭhānataṃ  saṅgaṇikāratassa, yaṃ phassayai phussayai (syā॰) sāmayikaṃ vimuttiṃ|

ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisamma, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

55.

 diṭṭhīvisūkāni upātivattau, pattau niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggau|

uppan‍nañāṇaumhi anañ‍ñanaiyyau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

56.

 nil‍laulupau  nik‍kuhau nippipāsau, nimmakkhau niddhantakasāvamauhau|

nirāsayau nirāsāsau (ka॰) sabbalaukai bhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

57.

 pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaitha, anatthadassiṃ visamai niviṭṭhaṃ|

sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

58.

 bahussutaṃ  dhammadharaṃ bhajaitha, mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhānavantaṃ|

añ‍ñāya atthāni vinaiyya kaṅkhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

59.

 khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ kāmasukhañ‍ca laukai, analaṅkaritvā anapaikkhamānau|

vibhūsanaṭṭhānā viratau sac‍cavādī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

60.

 puttañ‍ca dāraṃ pitarañ‍ca mātaraṃ, dhanāni dhañ‍ñāni ca bandhavāni bandhavāni ca (pī॰)|

hitvāna kāmāni yathaudhikāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

61.

 saṅgau  aisau parittamaittha saukhyaṃ, appassādau dukkhamaittha bhiyyau|

gaḷau aisau iti ñatvā mutīmā matīmā (syā॰ ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

62.

 sandālayitvāna padālayitvāna (ka॰) saṃyaujanāni, jālaṃva bhaitvā salilambucārī|

aggīva daḍḍhaṃ anivattamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

63.

 aukkhittacakkhū  na ca pādalaulau, guttindriyau rakkhitamānasānau|

anavassutau apariḍayhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

64.

 auhārayitvā  gihibyañ‍janāni, sañchan‍napattau sañchin‍napattau (syā॰ pī॰), pacchin‍napattau (ka॰) yathā pārichattau|

kāsāyavatthau  abhinikkhamitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

65.

 rasaisu gaidhaṃ akaraṃ alaulau, anañ‍ñapausī sapadānacārī|

kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittau appaṭibandhacittau (ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

66.

 pahāya  pañ‍cāvaraṇāni caitasau, upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbai|

anissitau chaitva chaitvā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) sinaihadausaṃ snaihadausaṃ (ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

67.

 vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca, pubbaiva ca saumanassadaumanassaṃ|

laddhānupaikkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

68.

 āraddhavīriyau paramatthapattiyā, alīnacittau akusītavutti|

daḷhanik‍kamau thāmabalūpapan‍nau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

69.

 paṭisal‍lānaṃ jhānamariñ‍camānau, dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārī|

ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisu, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

70.

 taṇhakkhayaṃ  patthayamappamattau, anaiḷamūgau anailamūgau (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) sutavā satīmā|

saṅkhātadhammau niyatau padhānavā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

71.

 sīhauva  saddaisu asantasantau, vātauva jālamhi asaj‍jamānau|

padumaṃva tauyaina alippamānau alimpamānau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

72.

 sīhau yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha, rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyya cārī|

saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

73.

 maittaṃ upaikkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ, āsaivamānau muditañ‍ca kālai|

sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

74.

 rāgañ‍ca  dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ, sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni|

asantasaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

75.

 bhajanti saivanti ca kāraṇatthā, nik‍kāraṇā dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittā|

attaṭṭhapañ‍ñā asucī manussā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

khaggavisāṇasuttaṃ tatiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. kasibhāradvājasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ  dakkhiṇagirismiṃ (ka॰) aikanāḷāyaṃ brāhmaṇagāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa pañ‍camattāni naṅgalasatāni payuttāni haunti vappakālai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa kammantau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa parivaisanā vattati| atha khau bhagavā yaina parivaisanā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi|

addasā khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ piṇḍāya ṭhitaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, samaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmi| tvampi, samaṇa, kasassu ca vapassu ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jassū’’ti|

‘‘ahampi khau, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| ‘‘na khau pana mayaṃ na khau pana samaṇa (syā॰) passāma bhautau gautamassa yugaṃ vā naṅgalaṃ vā phālaṃ vā pācanaṃ vā balibaddai balivaddai (sī॰ pī॰), balībaddai (?) vā| atha ca pana bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha – ‘ahampi khau, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi  ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmī’’’ti|

atha khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

76.

 ‘‘kassakau  paṭijānāsi, na ca passāma tai kasiṃ|

kasiṃ nau pucchitau brūhi, yathā jānaimu tai kasiṃ’’||

77.

 ‘‘saddhā bījaṃ tapau vuṭṭhi, pañ‍ñā mai yuganaṅgalaṃ|

hirī īsā manau yauttaṃ, sati mai phālapācanaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘kāyaguttau  vacīguttau, āhārai udarai yatau|

sac‍caṃ karaumi niddānaṃ, saurac‍caṃ mai pamaucanaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘vīriyaṃ mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

gacchati anivattantaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucati||

80.

 ‘‘aivamaisā  kasī kaṭṭhā, sā hauti amatapphalā|

aitaṃ kasiṃ kasitvāna, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

atha khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau mahatiyā kaṃsapātiyā pāyasaṃ pāyāsaṃ (sabbattha) vaḍḍhaitvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘bhuñ‍jatu bhavaṃ gautamau pāyasaṃ| kassakau bhavaṃ; yaṃ hi bhavaṃ gautamau amatapphalaṃ amatapphalampi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.197) kasiṃ kasatī’’ti|

81.

 ‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyyaṃ, sampassataṃ brāhmaṇa naisa dhammau|

gāthābhigītaṃ panudanti buddhā, dhammai satī brāhmaṇa vuttiraisā||

82.

 ‘‘añ‍ñaina  ca kaivalinaṃ mahaisiṃ, khīṇāsavaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cavūpasantaṃ|

an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhahassu, khaittaṃ hi taṃ puñ‍ñapaikkhassa hautī’’ti||

‘‘atha  kassa cāhaṃ, bhau gautama, imaṃ pāyasaṃ dammī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yassa sau pāyasau bhuttau sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā| taina hi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, taṃ pāyasaṃ appaharitai vā chaḍḍaihi appāṇakai vā udakai aupilāpaihī’’ti|

atha  khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau taṃ pāyasaṃ appāṇakai udakai aupilāpaisi| atha khau sau pāyasau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati sandhūmāyati sampadhūmāyati (syā॰)| saiyyathāpi nāma phālau divasaṃ santattau divasasantattau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati; aivamaiva sau pāyasau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati|

atha khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti dakkhintīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi  dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca, labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

alattha khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā bhāradvājau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca añ‍ñatarau ca khau (sī॰ pī॰), añ‍ñatarau khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausīti|

kasibhāradvājasuttaṃ catutthaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. cundasuttaṃ

83.

 ‘‘pucchāmi  muniṃ pahūtapañ‍ñaṃ, (iti cundau kammāraputtau)

buddhaṃ dhammassāmiṃ vītataṇhaṃ|

dvipaduttamaṃ dipaduttamaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sārathīnaṃ pavaraṃ, kati laukai samaṇā tadiṅgha brūhi’’||

84.

 ‘‘caturau  samaṇā na pañ‍camatthi, (cundāti bhagavā)

tai  tai āvikaraumi sakkhipuṭṭhau|

maggajinau maggadaisakau ca, maggai jīvati yau ca maggadūsī’’||

85.

 ‘‘kaṃ  maggajinaṃ vadanti buddhā, (iti cundau kammāraputtau)

maggakkhāyī kathaṃ atulyau hauti|

maggai jīvati mai brūhi puṭṭhau, atha mai āvikarauhi maggadūsiṃ’’ maggadūsī (ka॰)||

86.

 ‘‘yau tiṇṇakathaṃkathau visal‍lau, nibbānābhiratau anānugiddhau|

laukassa sadaivakassa naitā, tādiṃ maggajinaṃ vadanti buddhā||

87.

 ‘‘paramaṃ paramanti yaudha ñatvā, akkhāti  vibhajatai idhaiva dhammaṃ|

taṃ kaṅkhachidaṃ muniṃ anaijaṃ, dutiyaṃ bhikkhunamāhu maggadaisiṃ||

88.

 ‘‘yau dhammapadai sudaisitai, maggai jīvati sañ‍ñatau satīmā|

anavaj‍japadāni saivamānau, tatiyaṃ bhikkhunamāhu maggajīviṃ||

89.

 ‘‘chadanaṃ katvāna subbatānaṃ, pakkhandī kuladūsakau pagabbhau|

māyāvī asañ‍ñatau palāpau, patirūpaina caraṃ sa maggadūsī||

90.

 ‘‘aitai ca paṭivijjhi yau gahaṭṭhau, sutavā ariyasāvakau sapañ‍ñau|

sabbai  naitādisāti sabbai nai tādisāti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ñatvā, iti disvā na hāpaiti tassa saddhā|

kathaṃ hi duṭṭhaina asampaduṭṭhaṃ, suddhaṃ asuddhaina samaṃ karaiyyā’’ti||

cundasuttaṃ pañ‍camaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. parābhavasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

91.

 ‘‘parābhavantaṃ purisaṃ, mayaṃ pucchāma gautama gautamaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

bhagavantaṃ bhavantaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) puṭṭhumāgamma, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

92.

 ‘‘suvijānau bhavaṃ hauti, suvijānau duvijānau (syā॰ ka॰) parābhavau|

dhammakāmau bhavaṃ hauti, dhammadaissī parābhavau’’||

93.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, paṭhamau sau parābhavau|

dutiyaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

94.

 ‘‘asantassa piyā haunti, santai na kurutai piyaṃ|

asataṃ dhammaṃ raucaiti, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

95.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, dutiyau sau parābhavau|

tatiyaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

96.

 ‘‘niddāsīlī  sabhāsīlī, anuṭṭhātā ca yau narau|

alasau kaudhapañ‍ñāṇau, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

97.

 ‘‘iti  haitaṃ vijānāma, tatiyau sau parābhavau|

catutthaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

98.

 ‘‘yau  mātaraṃ yau mātaraṃ vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pitaraṃ vā, jiṇṇakaṃ gatayaubbanaṃ|

pahu santau na bharati, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

99.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, catutthau sau parābhavau|

pañ‍camaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

100.

 ‘‘yau brāhmaṇaṃ yau brāhmaṇaṃ vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samaṇaṃ vā, añ‍ñaṃ vāpi vanibbakaṃ|

musāvādaina vañ‍caiti, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

101.

 ‘‘iti  haitaṃ vijānāma, pañ‍camau sau parābhavau|

chaṭṭhamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

102.

 ‘‘pahūtavittau purisau, sahirañ‍ñau sabhaujanau|

aikau bhuñ‍jati sādūni, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

103.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, chaṭṭhamau sau parābhavau|

sattamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

104.

 ‘‘jātitthaddhau dhanatthaddhau, gauttatthaddhau ca yau narau|

sañ‍ñātiṃ atimañ‍ñaiti, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

105.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, sattamau sau parābhavau|

aṭṭhamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

106.

 ‘‘itthidhuttau surādhuttau, akkhadhuttau ca yau narau|

laddhaṃ laddhaṃ vināsaiti, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

107.

 ‘‘iti  haitaṃ vijānāma, aṭṭhamau sau parābhavau|

navamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

108.

 ‘‘saihi  dāraihi asantuṭṭhau dāraihyasantuṭṭhau (ka॰), vaisiyāsu padussati padissati (sī॰)|

dussati dissati (sī॰ pī॰) paradāraisu, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

109.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, navamau sau parābhavau|

dasamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

110.

 ‘‘atītayaubbanau pausau, ānaiti timbarutthaniṃ|

tassā issā na supati, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

111.

 ‘‘iti haitaṃ vijānāma, dasamau sau parābhavau|

aikādasamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

112.

 ‘‘itthiṃ sauṇḍiṃ vikiraṇiṃ, purisaṃ vāpi tādisaṃ|

issariyasmiṃ ṭhapaiti ṭhāpaiti (sī॰ pī॰), thapaiti (ka॰), taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

113.

 ‘‘iti  haitaṃ vijānāma, aikādasamau sau parābhavau|

dvādasamaṃ bhagavā brūhi, kiṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

114.

 ‘‘appabhaugau mahātaṇhau, khattiyai jāyatai kulai|

sau ca raj‍jaṃ patthayati, taṃ parābhavatau mukhaṃ’’||

115.

 ‘‘aitai parābhavai laukai, paṇḍitau samavaikkhiya|

ariyau dassanasampan‍nau, sa laukaṃ bhajatai siva’’nti||

parābhavasuttaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. vasalasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanai aggi paj‍jalitau hauti āhuti paggahitā| atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami|

addasā khau aggikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tatraiva atraiva (syā॰ ka॰), muṇḍaka; tatraiva, samaṇaka; tatraiva, vasalaka tiṭṭhāhī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā aggikabhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jānāsi pana tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vasalaṃ vā vasalakaraṇai vā dhammai’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhau gautama, jānāmi vasalaṃ vā vasalakaraṇai vā dhammai; sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu, yathāhaṃ jānaiyyaṃ vasalaṃ vā vasalakaraṇai vā dhammai’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau aggikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

116.

 ‘‘kaudhanau  upanāhī ca, pāpamakkhī ca yau narau|

vipan‍nadiṭṭhi  māyāvī, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

117.

 ‘‘aikajaṃ vā dvijaṃ dijaṃ (pī॰) vāpi, yaudha pāṇaṃ vihiṃsati|

yassa pāṇai dayā natthi, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

118.

 ‘‘yau  hanti parirundhati uparundhaiti (syā॰), uparundhati (ka॰), gāmāni nigamāni ca|

niggāhakau nigghātakau (?) samañ‍ñātau, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

119.

 ‘‘gāmai vā yadi vā rañ‍ñai, yaṃ paraisaṃ mamāyitaṃ|

thaiyyā adin‍namādaiti adin‍naṃ ādiyati (sī॰ pī॰), taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

120.

 ‘‘yau  havai iṇamādāya, cuj‍jamānau bhuñ‍jamānau (?) palāyati|

na hi tai iṇamatthīti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

121.

 ‘‘yau vai kiñ‍cikkhakamyatā, panthasmiṃ vajantaṃ janaṃ|

hantvā kiñ‍cikkhamādaiti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

122.

 ‘‘attahaitu parahaitu, dhanahaitu ca dhanahaitu va (ka॰) yau narau|

sakkhipuṭṭhau musā brūti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

123.

 ‘‘yau ñātīnaṃ sakhīnaṃ vā, dāraisu paṭidissati|

sāhasā sahasā (sī॰ syā॰) sampiyaina vā, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

124.

 ‘‘yau mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā, jiṇṇakaṃ gatayaubbanaṃ|

pahu santau na bharati, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

125.

 ‘‘yau mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā, bhātaraṃ bhaginiṃ sasuṃ|

hanti rausaiti vācāya, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

126.

 ‘‘yau atthaṃ pucchitau santau, anatthamanusāsati|

paṭicchan‍naina  mantaiti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

127.

 ‘‘yau katvā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti icchati vibha॰ 894 passitabbaṃ|

yau paṭicchan‍nakammantau, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

128.

 ‘‘yau  vai parakulaṃ gantvā, bhutvāna sutvā ca (syā॰ ka॰) sucibhaujanaṃ|

āgataṃ nappaṭipūjaiti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

129.

 ‘‘yau brāhmaṇaṃ samaṇaṃ vā, añ‍ñaṃ vāpi vanibbakaṃ|

musāvādaina vañ‍caiti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

130.

 ‘‘yau  brāhmaṇaṃ samaṇaṃ vā, bhattakālai upaṭṭhitai|

rausaiti vācā na ca daiti, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

131.

 ‘‘asataṃ yaudha pabrūti, mauhaina paliguṇṭhitau|

kiñ‍cikkhaṃ nijigīsānau nijigiṃsānau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

132.

 ‘‘yau cattānaṃ samuk‍kaṃsai, parai ca mavajānāti mavajānati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

nihīnau saina mānaina, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

133.

 ‘‘rausakau kadariyau ca, pāpicchau maccharī saṭhau|

ahirikau anauttappī, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

134.

 ‘‘yau buddhaṃ paribhāsati, atha vā tassa sāvakaṃ|

paribbājaṃ paribbajaṃ (ka॰), paribbājakaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) gahaṭṭhaṃ vā, taṃ jañ‍ñā vasalau iti||

135.

 ‘‘yau vai anarahaṃ anarahā (sī॰ pī॰) santau, arahaṃ paṭijānāti paṭijānati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

caurau sabrahmakai laukai, aisau khau vasalādhamau||

136.

 ‘‘aitai khau vasalā vuttā, mayā yaitai pakāsitā|

na  jac‍cā vasalau hauti, na jac‍cā hauti brāhmaṇau|

kammunā kammanā (sī॰ pī॰) vasalau hauti, kammunā hauti brāhmaṇau||

137.

 ‘‘tadamināpi jānātha, yathāmaidaṃ yathāpaidaṃ (ka॰) nidassanaṃ|

caṇḍālaputtau saupākau sapākau (?), mātaṅgau iti vissutau||

138.

 ‘‘sau  yasaṃ paramaṃ pattau sau yasapparamappattau (syā॰ ka॰), mātaṅgau yaṃ sudul‍labhaṃ|

āgacchuṃ tassupaṭṭhānaṃ, khattiyā brāhmaṇā bahū||

139.

 ‘‘daivayānaṃ  abhiruyha, virajaṃ sau mahāpathaṃ|

kāmarāgaṃ virājaitvā, brahmalaukūpagau ahu|

na naṃ jāti nivāraisi, brahmalaukūpapattiyā||

140.

 ‘‘ajjhāyakakulai jātā, brāhmaṇā mantabandhavā|

tai ca pāpaisu kammaisu, abhiṇhamupadissarai||

141.

 ‘‘diṭṭhaiva dhammai gārayhā, samparāyai ca duggati|

na nai jāti nivāraiti, duggatyā duggac‍cā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) garahāya vā||

142.

 ‘‘na jac‍cā vasalau hauti, na jac‍cā hauti brāhmaṇau|

kammunā vasalau hauti, kammunā hauti brāhmaṇau’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, aggikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰…  upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

vasalasuttaṃ sattamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. maittasuttaṃ

143.

 karaṇīyamatthakusalaina, yanta santaṃ padaṃ abhisamaic‍ca|

sak‍kau ujū ca suhujū sūjū (sī॰) ca, sūvacau cassa mudu anatimānī||

144.

 santussakau ca subharau ca, appakic‍cau ca sal‍lahukavutti|

santindriyau ca nipakau ca, appagabbhau kulaisvananugiddhau||

145.

 na  ca khuddamācarai kiñ‍ci, yaina viñ‍ñū parai upavadaiyyuṃ|

sukhinau va khaiminau hauntu, sabbasattā sabbai sattā (sī॰ syā॰) bhavantu sukhitattā||

146.

 yai  kaici pāṇabhūtatthi, tasā  vā thāvarā vanavasaisā|

dīghā vā yai va mahantā mahanta (?), majjhimā rassakā aṇukathūlā||

147.

 diṭṭhā  vā yai va adiṭṭhā adiṭṭha (?), yai va yai ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dūrai vasanti avidūrai|

bhūtā va sambhavaisī va bhūtā vā sambhavaisī vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), sabbasattā bhavantu sukhitattā||

148.

 na parau paraṃ nikubbaitha, nātimañ‍ñaitha katthaci na kañ‍ci naṃ kañ‍ci (sī॰ pī॰), naṃ kiñ‍ci (syā॰), na kiñ‍ci (ka॰)|

byārausanā paṭighasañ‍ñā, nāñ‍ñamañ‍ñassa dukkhamicchaiyya||

149.

 mātā yathā niyaṃ puttamāyusā aikaputtamanurakkhai|

aivampi sabbabhūtaisu, mānasaṃ bhāvayai aparimāṇaṃ||

150.

 maittañ‍ca sabbalaukasmi, mānasaṃ bhāvayai aparimāṇaṃ|

uddhaṃ adhau ca tiriyañ‍ca, asambādhaṃ avairamasapattaṃ||

151.

 tiṭṭhaṃ  caraṃ nisin‍nau va vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sayānau  yāvatāssa vitamiddhau vigatamiddhau (bahūsu)|

aitaṃ satiṃ adhiṭṭhaiyya, brahmamaitaṃ vihāramidhamāhu||

152.

 diṭṭhiñ‍ca anupaggamma, sīlavā dassanaina sampan‍nau|

kāmaisu vinaya vinaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gaidhaṃ, na hi jātuggabbhasaiyya punaraitīti||

maittasuttaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. haimavatasuttaṃ

153.

 ‘‘aj‍ja  pan‍narasau upausathau, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

dibbā dibyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ratti upaṭṭhitā|

anaumanāmaṃ satthāraṃ, handa passāma gautamaṃ’’||

154.

 ‘‘kac‍ci  manau supaṇihitau, (iti haimavatau yakkhau)

sabbabhūtaisu tādinau|

kac‍ci iṭṭhai aniṭṭhai ca, saṅkappassa vasīkatā’’||

155.

 ‘‘manau cassa supaṇihitau, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

sabbabhūtaisu tādinau|

athau iṭṭhai aniṭṭhai ca, saṅkappassa vasīkatā’’||

156.

 ‘‘kac‍ci  adin‍naṃ nādiyati, (iti haimavatau yakkhau)

kac‍ci pāṇaisu sañ‍ñatau|

kac‍ci ārā pamādamhā, kac‍ci jhānaṃ na riñ‍cati’’||

157.

 ‘‘na  sau adin‍naṃ ādiyati, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

athau pāṇaisu sañ‍ñatau|

athau ārā pamādamhā, buddhau jhānaṃ na riñ‍cati’’||

158.

 ‘‘kac‍ci musā na bhaṇati, (iti haimavatau yakkhau)

kac‍ci na khīṇabyappathau|

kac‍ci  vaibhūtiyaṃ nāha, kac‍ci samphaṃ na bhāsati’’||

159.

 ‘‘musā ca sau na bhaṇati, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

athau na khīṇabyappathau|

athau vaibhūtiyaṃ nāha, mantā atthaṃ ca atthaṃ sau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhāsati’’||

160.

 ‘‘kac‍ci na raj‍jati kāmaisu, (iti haimavatau yakkhau)

kac‍ci cittaṃ anāvilaṃ|

kac‍ci mauhaṃ atik‍kantau, kac‍ci dhammaisu cakkhumā’’||

161.

 ‘‘na  sau raj‍jati kāmaisu, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

athau cittaṃ anāvilaṃ|

sabbamauhaṃ atik‍kantau, buddhau dhammaisu cakkhumā’’||

162.

 ‘‘kac‍ci vij‍jāya sampan‍nau, (iti haimavatau yakkhau )

kac‍ci saṃsuddhacāraṇau|

kac‍cissa  āsavā khīṇā, kac‍ci natthi punabbhavau’’||

163.

 ‘‘vij‍jāya  caiva sampan‍nau, (iti sātāgirau yakkhau)

athau saṃsuddhacāraṇau|

sabbassa āsavā khīṇā, natthi tassa punabbhavau’’||

164.

 ‘‘sampan‍naṃ  muninau cittaṃ, kammunā byappathaina ca|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍naṃ, dhammatau naṃ pasaṃsati’’||

165.

 ‘‘sampan‍naṃ muninau cittaṃ, kammunā byappathaina ca|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍naṃ, dhammatau anumaudasi’’||

166.

 ‘‘sampan‍naṃ muninau cittaṃ, kammunā byappathaina ca|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍naṃ, handa passāma gautamaṃ||

167.

 ‘‘aiṇijaṅghaṃ kisaṃ vīraṃ dhīraṃ (syā॰), appāhāraṃ alaulupaṃ|

muniṃ vanasmiṃ jhāyantaṃ, aihi passāma gautamaṃ||

168.

 ‘‘sīhaṃvaikacaraṃ nāgaṃ, kāmaisu anapaikkhinaṃ|

upasaṅkamma pucchāma, mac‍cupāsappamaucanaṃ||

169.

 ‘‘akkhātāraṃ pavattāraṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ|

buddhaṃ vairabhayātītaṃ, mayaṃ pucchāma gautamaṃ’’||

170.

 ‘‘kismiṃ  laukau samuppan‍nau, (iti haimavatau yakkhau)

kismiṃ kubbati santhavaṃ sandhavaṃ (ka॰)|

kissa laukau upādāya, kismiṃ laukau vihañ‍ñati’’||

171.

 ‘‘chasu  chassu (sī॰ pī॰) laukau samuppan‍nau, (haimavatāti bhagavā)

chasu kubbati santhavaṃ|

chan‍namaiva upādāya, chasu laukau vihañ‍ñati’’||

172.

 ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ upādānaṃ, yattha laukau vihañ‍ñati|

niyyānaṃ pucchitau brūhi, kathaṃ dukkhā pamuc‍cati’’ pamuñ‍cati (syā॰)||

173.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā laukai, manauchaṭṭhā pavaiditā|

aittha chandaṃ virājaitvā, aivaṃ dukkhā pamuc‍cati||

174.

 ‘‘aitaṃ laukassa niyyānaṃ, akkhātaṃ vau yathātathaṃ|

aitaṃ vau ahamakkhāmi, aivaṃ dukkhā pamuc‍cati’’||

175.

 ‘‘kau  sūdha tarati aughaṃ, kaudha tarati aṇṇavaṃ|

appatiṭṭhai anālambai, kau gambhīrai na sīdati’’||

176.

 ‘‘sabbadā sīlasampan‍nau, pañ‍ñavā susamāhitau|

ajjhattacintī ajjhattasañ‍ñī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) satimā, aughaṃ tarati duttaraṃ||

177.

 ‘‘viratau kāmasañ‍ñāya, sabbasaṃyaujanātigau|

nandībhavaparikkhīṇau, sau gambhīrai na sīdati’’||

178.

 ‘‘gabbhīrapañ‍ñaṃ nipuṇatthadassiṃ, akiñ‍canaṃ kāmabhavai asattaṃ|

taṃ passatha sabbadhi vippamuttaṃ, dibbai pathai kamamānaṃ mahaisiṃ||

179.

 ‘‘anaumanāmaṃ  nipuṇatthadassiṃ, pañ‍ñādadaṃ  kāmālayai asattaṃ|

taṃ  passatha sabbaviduṃ sumaidhaṃ, ariyai pathai kamamānaṃ mahaisiṃ||

180.

 ‘‘sudiṭṭhaṃ vata nau aj‍ja, suppabhātaṃ suhuṭṭhitaṃ|

yaṃ addasāma sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

181.

 ‘‘imai dasasatā yakkhā, iddhimantau yasassinau|

sabbai taṃ saraṇaṃ yanti, tvaṃ nau satthā anuttarau||

182.

 ‘‘tai mayaṃ vicarissāma, gāmā gāmaṃ nagā nagaṃ|

namassamānā sambuddhaṃ, dhammassa ca sudhammata’’nti||

haimavatasuttaṃ navamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. āḷavakasuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati āḷavakassa yakkhassa bhavanai| atha khau āḷavakau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti  bhagavā nikkhami| ‘‘pavisa, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā pāvisi|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti  bhagavā nikkhami| ‘‘pavisa, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā pāvisi|

catutthampi khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, nikkhamissāmi| yaṃ tai karaṇīyaṃ, taṃ karauhī’’ti|

‘‘pañhaṃ  taṃ, samaṇa, pucchissāmi| sacai mai na byākarissasi, cittaṃ vā tai khipissāmi, hadayaṃ vā tai phālaissāmi, pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipissāmī’’ti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yau mai cittaṃ vā khipaiyya hadayaṃ vā phālaiyya pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipaiyya| api ca tvaṃ, āvusau, puccha yadākaṅkhasī’’ti| atha khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

183.

 ‘‘kiṃ sūdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, kiṃ su suciṇṇaṃ sukhamāvahāti|

kiṃ su kiṃ sū (sī॰) havai sādutaraṃ rasānaṃ, kathaṃ jīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭhaṃ’’||

184.

 ‘‘saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahāti|

sac‍caṃ havai sādutaraṃ rasānaṃ, pañ‍ñājīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭhaṃ’’||

185.

 ‘‘kathaṃ su tarati aughaṃ, kathaṃ su tarati aṇṇavaṃ|

kathaṃ  su dukkhamac‍caiti, kathaṃ su parisujjhati’’||

186.

 ‘‘saddhā  tarati aughaṃ, appamādaina aṇṇavaṃ|

vīriyaina viriyaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dukkhamac‍caiti, pañ‍ñāya parisujjhati’’||

187.

 ‘‘kathaṃ  su labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ, kathaṃ su vindatai dhanaṃ|

kathaṃ su kittiṃ pappauti, kathaṃ mittāni ganthati|

asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ, kathaṃ paic‍ca na saucati’’||

188.

 ‘‘saddahānau arahataṃ, dhammaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

sussūsaṃ sussūsā (sī॰ pī॰) labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ, appamattau vicakkhaṇau||

189.

 ‘‘patirūpakārī dhuravā, uṭṭhātā vindatai dhanaṃ|

sac‍caina kittiṃ pappauti, dadaṃ mittāni ganthati||

190.

 ‘‘yassaitai  caturau dhammā, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

sac‍caṃ dhammau damau (?) dhiti cāgau, sa vai paic‍ca na saucati||

191.

 ‘‘iṅgha añ‍ñaipi pucchassu, puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇai|

yadi sac‍cā damā cāgā, khantyā bhiyyaudha vij‍jati’’||

192.

 ‘‘kathaṃ nu dāni pucchaiyyaṃ, puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇai|

yauhaṃ sauhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aj‍ja pajānāmi, yau atthau samparāyikau||

193.

 ‘‘atthāya vata mai buddhau, vāsāyāḷavimāgamā|

yauhaṃ aṭṭhinhārūhi saṃyuttau (syā॰ ka॰) aj‍ja pajānāmi, yattha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ||

194.

 ‘‘sau ahaṃ vicarissāmi, gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ|

namassamānau sambuddhaṃ, dhammassa ca sudhammata’’nti||

āḷavakasuttaṃ dasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. vijayasuttaṃ

195.

 caraṃ  vā yadi vā tiṭṭhaṃ, nisin‍nau uda vā sayaṃ|

samiñ‍jaiti pasāraiti, aisā kāyassa iñ‍janā||

196.

 aṭṭhinahārusaṃyuttau, tacamaṃsāvalaipanau|

chaviyā kāyau paṭicchan‍nau, yathābhūtaṃ na dissati||

197.

 antapūrau  udarapūrau, yakanapaiḷassa yakapaiḷassa (sī॰ syā॰) vatthinau|

hadayassa papphāsassa, vak‍kassa pihakassa ca||

198.

 siṅghāṇikāya  khaiḷassa, saidassa ca maidassa ca|

lauhitassa lasikāya, pittassa ca vasāya ca||

199.

 athassa navahi sautaihi, asucī savati sabbadā|

akkhimhā akkhigūthakau, kaṇṇamhā kaṇṇagūthakau||

200.

 siṅghāṇikā ca nāsatau, mukhaina vamataikadā|

pittaṃ saimhañ‍ca vamati, kāyamhā saidajal‍likā||

201.

 athassa susiraṃ sīsaṃ, matthaluṅgassa pūritaṃ|

subhatau naṃ mañ‍ñati, bālau avij‍jāya purakkhatau||

202.

 yadā ca sau matau saiti, uddhumātau vinīlakau|

apaviddhau susānasmiṃ, anapaikkhā haunti ñātayau||

203.

 khādanti naṃ suvānā supāṇā (pī॰) ca, siṅgālā sigālā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vakā kimī|

kākā  gijjhā ca khādanti, yai cañ‍ñai santi pāṇinau||

204.

 sutvāna  buddhavacanaṃ, bhikkhu pañ‍ñāṇavā idha|

sau khau naṃ parijānāti, yathābhūtañhi passati||

205.

 yathā idaṃ tathā aitaṃ, yathā aitaṃ tathā idaṃ|

ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca, kāyai chandaṃ virājayai||

206.

 chandarāgavirattau sau, bhikkhu pañ‍ñāṇavā idha|

ajjhagā amataṃ santiṃ, nibbānaṃ padamac‍cutaṃ||

207.

 dvipādakauyaṃ dipādakauyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) asuci, duggandhau parihārati parihīrati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

nānākuṇapaparipūrau, vissavantau tatau tatau||

208.

 aitādisaina  kāyaina, yau mañ‍ñai uṇṇamaitavai un‍namaitavai (?)|

paraṃ vā avajānaiyya, kimañ‍ñatra adassanāti||

vijayasuttaṃ aikādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. munisuttaṃ

209.

 santhavātau  sandhavatau (ka॰) bhayaṃ jātaṃ, nikaitā jāyatai rajau|

anikaitamasanthavaṃ, aitaṃ vai munidassanaṃ||

210.

 yau jātamucchij‍ja na raupayaiyya, jāyantamassa nānuppavaicchai|

tamāhu aikaṃ muninaṃ carantaṃ, addakkhi  sau santipadaṃ mahaisi||

211.

 saṅkhāya  vatthūni pamāya pahāya (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰), samāya (ka॰) pa + mī + tvā = pamāya, yathā nissāyātipadaṃ bījaṃ, sinaihamassa nānuppavaicchai|

sa vai munī jātikhayantadassī, tak‍kaṃ pahāya na upaiti saṅkhaṃ||

212.

 añ‍ñāya sabbāni nivaisanāni, anikāmayaṃ añ‍ñatarampi taisaṃ|

sa vai munī vītagaidhau agiddhau, nāyūhatī pāragatau hi hauti||

213.

 sabbābhibhuṃ sabbaviduṃ sumaidhaṃ, sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittaṃ|

sabbañ‍jahaṃ taṇhakkhayai vimuttaṃ, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni muniṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vaidayanti||

214.

 pañ‍ñābalaṃ sīlavatūpapan‍naṃ, samāhitaṃ jhānarataṃ satīmaṃ|

saṅgā pamuttaṃ akhilaṃ anāsavaṃ, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

215.

 aikaṃ  carantaṃ munimappamattaṃ, nindāpasaṃsāsu avaidhamānaṃ|

sīhaṃva saddaisu asantasantaṃ, vātaṃva  jālamhi asaj‍jamānaṃ|

padmaṃva  padumaṃva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tauyaina alippamānaṃ alimpamānaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), naitāramañ‍ñaisamanañ‍ñanaiyyaṃ|

taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

216.

 yau  augahaṇai thambhaurivābhijāyati, yasmiṃ parai vācāpariyantaṃ vācaṃ pariyantaṃ (ka॰) vadanti|

taṃ vītarāgaṃ susamāhitindriyaṃ, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

217.

 yau vai ṭhitattau tasaraṃva uj‍ju, jigucchati kammaihi pāpakaihi|

vīmaṃsamānau visamaṃ samañ‍ca, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

218.

 yau sañ‍ñatattau na karauti pāpaṃ, daharau majjhimau ca muni daharau ca majjhau ca munī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yatattau|

arausanaiyyau na sau rausaiti kañ‍ci na rausaiti (syā॰), taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

219.

 yadaggatau majjhatau saisatau vā, piṇḍaṃ labhaitha paradattūpajīvī|

nālaṃ thutuṃ naupi nipac‍cavādī, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

220.

 muniṃ  carantaṃ virataṃ maithunasmā, yau yaubbanai naupanibajjhatai k‍vaci|

madappamādā virataṃ vippamuttaṃ, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

221.

 añ‍ñāya  laukaṃ paramatthadassiṃ, aughaṃ samuddaṃ atitariya tādiṃ|

taṃ  chin‍naganthaṃ asitaṃ anāsavaṃ, taṃ vāpi dhīrā muni vaidayanti||

222.

 asamā ubhau dūravihāravuttinau, gihī gihi (ka॰) dārapausī amamau ca subbatau|

parapāṇaraudhāya gihī asañ‍ñatau, nic‍caṃ munī rakkhati pāṇinai pāṇinau (sī॰) yatau||

223.

 sikhī yathā nīlagīvau nīlagivau (syā॰) vihaṅgamau, haṃsassa naupaiti javaṃ kudācanaṃ|

aivaṃ gihī nānukarauti bhikkhunau, muninau vivittassa vanamhi jhāyatauti||

munisuttaṃ dvādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

uragavaggau paṭhamau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

uragau dhaniyau caiva, visāṇañ‍ca tathā kasi|

cundau parābhavau  caiva, vasalau maittabhāvanā||

sātāgirau āḷavakau, vijayau ca tathā muni|

dvādasaitāni suttāni, uragavaggauti vuc‍catīti||


2. cūḷavaggau

1. ratanasuttaṃ

224.

 yānīdha  bhūtāni samāgatāni, bhummāni bhūmāni (ka॰) vā yāni va antalikkhai|

sabbaiva bhūtā sumanā bhavantu, athaupi sak‍kac‍ca suṇantu bhāsitaṃ||

225.

 tasmā hi bhūtā nisāmaitha sabbai, maittaṃ karautha mānusiyā pajāya|

divā ca rattau ca haranti yai baliṃ, tasmā hi nai rakkhatha appamattā||

226.

 yaṃ kiñ‍ci vittaṃ idha vā huraṃ vā, saggaisu vā yaṃ ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ|

na nau samaṃ atthi tathāgataina, idampi buddhai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ|

aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

227.

 khayaṃ virāgaṃ amataṃ paṇītaṃ, yadajjhagā  sakyamunī samāhitau|

na taina dhammaina samatthi kiñ‍ci, idampi dhammai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ|

aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

228.

 yaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhau parivaṇṇayī suciṃ, samādhimānantarikañ‍ñamāhu|

samādhinā  taina samau na vij‍jati, idampi dhammai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ|

aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

229.

 yai  puggalā aṭṭha sataṃ pasatthā, cattāri aitāni yugāni haunti|

tai dakkhiṇaiyyā sugatassa sāvakā, aitaisu din‍nāni mahapphalāni|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina  sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

230.

 yai  suppayuttā manasā daḷhaina, nik‍kāminau gautamasāsanamhi|

tai pattipattā amataṃ vigayha, laddhā mudhā nibbutiṃ nibbuti (ka॰) bhuñ‍jamānā|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

231.

 yathindakhīlau pathavissitau padavissitau (ka॰ sī॰), paṭhaviṃ sitau (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) siyā, catubbhi vātaihi asampakampiyau|

tathūpamaṃ sappurisaṃ vadāmi, yau ariyasac‍cāni avaic‍ca passati|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

232.

 yai ariyasac‍cāni vibhāvayanti, gambhīrapañ‍ñaina sudaisitāni|

kiñ‍cāpi tai haunti bhusaṃ pamattā, na tai bhavaṃ aṭṭhamamādiyanti|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina  sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

233.

 sahāvassa dassanasampadāya sahāvasaddassanasampadāya (ka॰), tayassu dhammā jahitā bhavanti|

sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi  vicikicchitañ‍ca, sīlabbataṃ vāpi yadatthi kiñ‍ci||

234.

 catūhapāyaihi  ca vippamuttau, chac‍cābhiṭhānāni cha cābhiṭhānāni (sī॰ syā॰) bhabba kātuṃ abhabbau kātuṃ (sī॰)|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

235.

 kiñ‍cāpi sau kamma kammaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) karauti pāpakaṃ, kāyaina vācā uda caitasā vā|

abhabba abhabbau (bahūsu) sau tassa paṭicchadāya paṭicchādāya (sī॰), abhabbatā diṭṭhapadassa vuttā|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

236.

 vanappagumbai yatha yathā (sī॰ syā॰) phussitaggai, gimhānamāsai paṭhamasmiṃ paṭhamasmi (?) gimhai|

tathūpamaṃ dhammavaraṃ adaisayi adaisayī (sī॰), nibbānagāmiṃ paramaṃ hitāya|

idampi buddhai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

237.

 varau varañ‍ñū varadau varāharau, anuttarau dhammavaraṃ adaisayi|

idampi buddhai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

238.

 khīṇaṃ  purāṇaṃ nava natthi sambhavaṃ, virattacittāyatikai bhavasmiṃ|

tai khīṇabījā avirūḷhichandā, nibbanti  dhīrā yathāyaṃ yathayaṃ (ka॰) padīpau|

idampi saṅghai ratanaṃ paṇītaṃ, aitaina sac‍caina suvatthi hautu||

239.

 yānīdha  bhūtāni samāgatāni, bhummāni vā yāni va antalikkhai|

tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ, buddhaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautu||

240.

 yānīdha bhūtāni samāgatāni, bhummāni vā yāni va antalikkhai|

tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ, dhammaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautu||

241.

 yānīdha bhūtāni samāgatāni, bhummāni vā yāni va antalikkhai|

tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ, saṅghaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautūti||

ratanasuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. āmagandhasuttaṃ

242.

 ‘‘sāmākaciṅgūlakacīnakāni  ca, pattapphalaṃ mūlaphalaṃ gavipphalaṃ|

dhammaina laddhaṃ satamasnamānā satamasamānā (sī॰ pī॰), satamassamānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), na kāmakāmā alikaṃ bhaṇanti||

243.

 ‘‘yadasnamānau sukataṃ suniṭṭhitaṃ, paraihi din‍naṃ payataṃ paṇītaṃ|

sālīnaman‍naṃ  paribhuñ‍jamānau, sau bhuñ‍jasī kassapa āmagandhaṃ||

244.

 ‘‘na  āmagandhau mama kappatīti, ic‍caiva tvaṃ bhāsasi brahmabandhu|

sālīnaman‍naṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau, sakuntamaṃsaihi susaṅkhataihi|

pucchāmi taṃ kassapa aitamatthaṃ, kathaṃ pakārau tava āmagandhau’’||

245.

 ‘‘pāṇātipātau  vadhachaidabandhanaṃ, thaiyyaṃ musāvādau nikativañ‍canāni ca|

ajjhainakuttaṃ ajjhaina kuj‍jaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) paradārasaivanā, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

246.

 ‘‘yai  idha kāmaisu asañ‍ñatā janā, rasaisu giddhā asucibhāvamassitā asucīkamissitā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

natthikadiṭṭhī visamā duran‍nayā, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

247.

 ‘‘yai lūkhasā dāruṇā piṭṭhimaṃsikā yai lūkharasā dāruṇā parapiṭṭhimaṃsikā (ka॰), mittaddunau nik‍karuṇātimāninau|

adānasīlā na ca dainti kassaci, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

248.

 ‘‘kaudhau  madau thambhau pac‍cupaṭṭhāpanā pac‍cuṭṭhāpanā ca (sī॰ syā॰), pac‍cuṭṭhāpanā (pī॰), māyā usūyā bhassasamussayau ca|

mānātimānau ca asabbhi santhavau, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

249.

 ‘‘yai pāpasīlā iṇaghātasūcakā, vauhārakūṭā idha pāṭirūpikā pātirūpikā (?)|

narādhamā yaidha karaunti kibbisaṃ, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

250.

 ‘‘yai  idha pāṇaisu asañ‍ñatā janā, paraisamādāya vihaisamuyyutā|

dussīlaluddā pharusā anādarā, aisāmagandhau  na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

251.

 ‘‘aitaisu giddhā viruddhātipātinau, nic‍cuyyutā paic‍ca tamaṃ vajanti yai|

patanti sattā nirayaṃ avaṃsirā, aisāmagandhau na hi maṃsabhaujanaṃ||

252.

 ‘‘na macchamaṃsānamanāsakattaṃ na macchamaṃsaṃ na anāsakattaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha mūlapāṭhau), na maṃcchamaṃsānānāsakattaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), na naggiyaṃ na muṇḍiyaṃ jaṭājal‍laṃ|

kharājināni nāggihuttassupasaivanā, yai vāpi laukai amarā bahū tapā|

mantāhutī yañ‍ñamutūpasaivanā, saudhainti mac‍caṃ avitiṇṇakaṅkhaṃ||

253.

 ‘‘yau taisu  sautaisu (sī॰ pī॰) guttau viditindriyau carai, dhammai ṭhitau aj‍javamaddavai ratau|

saṅgātigau sabbadukkhappahīnau, na lippati na limpati (syā॰ kaṃ ka॰) diṭṭhasutaisu dhīrau’’||

254.

 ic‍caitamatthaṃ bhagavā punappunaṃ, akkhāsi naṃ taṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vaidayi mantapāragū|

citrāhi gāthāhi munī pakāsayi, nirāmagandhau asitau duran‍nayau||

255.

 sutvāna  buddhassa subhāsitaṃ padaṃ, nirāmagandhaṃ  sabbadukkhappanūdanaṃ|

nīcamanau vandi tathāgatassa, tatthaiva pabbaj‍jamaraucayitthāti||

āmagandhasuttaṃ dutiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. hirisuttaṃ

256.

 hiriṃ  tarantaṃ vijigucchamānaṃ, tavāhamasmi sakhāhamasmi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) iti bhāsamānaṃ|

sayhāni kammāni anādiyantaṃ, naisau mamanti iti naṃ vijañ‍ñā||

257.

 ananvayaṃ atthanvayaṃ (ka॰) piyaṃ vācaṃ, yau mittaisu pakubbati|

akarauntaṃ bhāsamānaṃ, parijānanti paṇḍitā||

258.

 na  sau mittau yau sadā appamattau, bhaidāsaṅkī randhamaivānupassī|

yasmiñ‍ca saiti urasīva puttau, sa vai mittau yau paraihi abhaij‍jau||

259.

 pāmuj‍jakaraṇaṃ ṭhānaṃ, pasaṃsāvahanaṃ sukhaṃ|

phalānisaṃsau bhāvaiti, vahantau paurisaṃ dhuraṃ||

260.

 pavivaikarasaṃ pitvā, rasaṃ upasamassa ca|

niddarau  hauti nippāpau, dhammapītirasaṃ pivanti||

hirisuttaṃ tatiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. maṅgalasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

261.

 ‘‘bahū daivā manussā ca, maṅgalāni acintayuṃ|

ākaṅkhamānā sautthānaṃ, brūhi maṅgalamuttamaṃ’’||

262.

 ‘‘asaivanā  ca bālānaṃ, paṇḍitānañ‍ca saivanā|

pūjā ca pūjanaiyyānaṃ pūjanīyānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

263.

 ‘‘patirūpadaisavāsau ca, pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñatā|

attasammāpaṇidhi attasammāpaṇīdhī (katthaci) ca, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

264.

 ‘‘bāhusac‍cañ‍ca  sippañ‍ca, vinayau ca susikkhitau|

subhāsitā ca yā vācā, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

265.

 ‘‘mātāpitu upaṭṭhānaṃ, puttadārassa saṅgahau|

anākulā  ca kammantā, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

266.

 ‘‘dānañ‍ca dhammacariyā ca, ñātakānañ‍ca saṅgahau|

anavaj‍jāni kammāni, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

267.

 ‘‘āratī viratī pāpā, maj‍japānā ca saṃyamau|

appamādau ca dhammaisu, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

268.

 ‘‘gāravau  ca nivātau ca, santuṭṭhi ca katañ‍ñutā|

kālaina dhammassavanaṃ dhammasavaṇaṃ (katthaci), dhammasavanaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

269.

 ‘‘khantī ca sauvacassatā, samaṇānañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

kālaina dhammasākacchā, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

270.

 ‘‘tapau ca brahmacariyañ‍ca, ariyasac‍cāna dassanaṃ|

nibbānasacchikiriyā ca, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

271.

 ‘‘phuṭṭhassa laukadhammaihi, cittaṃ yassa na kampati|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ, aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ||

272.

 ‘‘aitādisāni katvāna, sabbatthamaparājitā|

sabbattha sautthiṃ gacchanti, taṃ taisaṃ maṅgalamuttama’’nti||

maṅgalasuttaṃ catutthaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. sūcilaumasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati ṭaṅkitamañ‍cai sūcilaumassa yakkhassa bhavanai| taina khau pana samayaina  kharau ca yakkhau sūcilaumau ca yakkhau bhagavatau avidūrai atik‍kamanti| atha khau kharau yakkhau sūcilaumaṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisau samaṇau’’ti| ‘‘naisau samaṇau, samaṇakau aisau| yāvāhaṃ jānāmi yāva jānāmi (sī॰ pī॰) yadi vā sau samaṇau yadi vā samaṇau (syā॰), yadi vā sau samaṇakau’’ti yadi vā samaṇakauti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau kāyaṃ upanāmaisi| atha khau bhagavā kāyaṃ apanāmaisi| atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘bhāyasi maṃ, samaṇā’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, bhāyāmi; api ca tai sapphassau pāpakau’’ti|

‘‘pañhaṃ taṃ, samaṇa, pucchissāmi| sacai mai na byākarissasi, cittaṃ vā tai khipissāmi, hadayaṃ vā tai phālaissāmi, pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipissāmī’’ti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yau mai cittaṃ vā khipaiyya hadayaṃ vā phālaiyya pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipaiyya| api ca tvaṃ, āvusau, puccha yadākaṅkhasī’’ti| atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

273.

 ‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca kutaunidānā, aratī  ratī laumahaṃsau kutaujā|

kutau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajanti’’||

274.

 ‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca itaunidānā, aratī ratī laumahaṃsau itaujā|

itau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajanti||

274.

 ‘‘snaihajā  attasambhūtā, nigraudhassaiva khandhajā|

puthū visattā kāmaisu, māluvāva vitatāvanai||

275.

 ‘‘yai naṃ pajānanti yataunidānaṃ, tai naṃ vinaudainti suṇauhi yakkha|

tai duttaraṃ aughamimaṃ taranti, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti||

sūcilaumasuttaṃ pañ‍camaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. dhammacariyasuttaṃ

276.

 dhammacariyaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, aitadāhu vasuttamaṃ|

pabbajitaupi cai hauti, agārā anagāriyaṃ||

277.

 sau cai mukharajātikau, vihaisābhiratau magau|

jīvitaṃ  tassa pāpiyau, rajaṃ vaḍḍhaiti attanau||

278.

 kalahābhiratau bhikkhu, mauhadhammaina āvutau|

akkhātampi na jānāti, dhammaṃ buddhaina daisitaṃ||

279.

 vihaisaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ, avij‍jāya purakkhatau|

saṃkilaisaṃ na jānāti, maggaṃ nirayagāminaṃ||

280.

 vinipātaṃ samāpan‍nau, gabbhā gabbhaṃ tamā tamaṃ|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, paic‍ca dukkhaṃ nigacchati||

281.

 gūthakūpau yathā assa, sampuṇṇau gaṇavassikau|

yau ca aivarūpau assa, dubbisaudhau hi sāṅgaṇau||

282.

 yaṃ aivarūpaṃ jānātha, bhikkhavau gaihanissitaṃ|

pāpicchaṃ pāpasaṅkappaṃ, pāpaācāragaucaraṃ||

283.

 sabbai  samaggā hutvāna, abhinibbaj‍jiyātha abhinibbaj‍jayātha (sī॰ pī॰ a॰ ni॰ 8.10) naṃ|

kāraṇḍavaṃ kāraṇḍaṃ va (syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.10 niddhamatha, kasambuṃ apakassatha avakassatha (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰)||

284.

 tatau palāpai palāsai (ka॰) vāhaitha, assamaṇai samaṇamāninai|

niddhamitvāna pāpicchai, pāpaācāragaucarai||

285.

 suddhā  suddhaihi saṃvāsaṃ, kappayavhau patissatā|

tatau samaggā nipakā, dukkhassantaṃ karissathāti||

dhammacariyasuttaṃ kapilasuttaṃ (aṭṭha॰) chaṭṭhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. brāhmaṇadhammikasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā kausalakā brāhmaṇamahāsālā jiṇṇā vuḍḍhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai brāhmaṇamahāsālā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sandissanti nu khau, bhau gautama, aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇadhammai’’ti? ‘‘na khau, brāhmaṇā, sandissanti aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇadhammai’’ti| ‘‘sādhu nau bhavaṃ gautamau paurāṇānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇadhammaṃ bhāsatu, sacai bhautau gautamassa agarū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇā, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau tai brāhmaṇamahāsālā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

286.

 ‘‘isayau pubbakā āsuṃ, sañ‍ñatattā tapassinau|

pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai hitvā, attadatthamacārisuṃ||

287.

 ‘‘na  pasū brāhmaṇānāsuṃ, na hirañ‍ñaṃ na dhāniyaṃ|

sajjhāyadhanadhañ‍ñāsuṃ, brahmaṃ nidhimapālayuṃ||

288.

 ‘‘yaṃ  naisaṃ pakataṃ āsi, dvārabhattaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ|

saddhāpakatamaisānaṃ, dātavai tadamañ‍ñisuṃ||

289.

 ‘‘nānārattaihi  vatthaihi, sayanaihāvasathaihi ca|

phītā janapadā raṭṭhā, tai namassiṃsu brāhmaṇai||

290.

 ‘‘avajjhā brāhmaṇā āsuṃ, ajaiyyā dhammarakkhitā|

na nai kauci nivāraisi, kuladvāraisu sabbasau||

291.

 ‘‘aṭṭhacattālīsaṃ vassāni, (kaumāra) brahmacariyaṃ cariṃsu tai|

vij‍jācaraṇapariyaiṭṭhiṃ, acaruṃ brāhmaṇā purai||

292.

 ‘‘na brāhmaṇā añ‍ñamagamuṃ, napi bhariyaṃ kiṇiṃsu tai|

sampiyainaiva saṃvāsaṃ, saṅgantvā samaraucayuṃ||

293.

 ‘‘añ‍ñatra tamhā samayā, utuvairamaṇiṃ pati|

antarā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ, nāssu gacchanti brāhmaṇā||

294.

 ‘‘brahmacariyañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, aj‍javaṃ maddavaṃ tapaṃ|

saurac‍caṃ avihiṃsañ‍ca, khantiñ‍cāpi avaṇṇayuṃ||

295.

 ‘‘yau  naisaṃ paramau āsi, brahmā daḷhaparak‍kamau|

sa vāpi maithunaṃ dhammaṃ, supinantaipi nāgamā||

296.

 ‘‘tassa vattamanusikkhantā, idhaikai viñ‍ñujātikā|

brahmacariyañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, khantiñ‍cāpi avaṇṇayuṃ||

297.

 ‘‘taṇḍulaṃ sayanaṃ vatthaṃ, sappitailañ‍ca yāciya|

dhammaina samaudhānaitvā, tatau yañ‍ñamakappayuṃ||

298.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhitasmiṃ  yañ‍ñasmiṃ, nāssu gāvau haniṃsu tai|

yathā mātā pitā bhātā, añ‍ñai vāpi ca ñātakā|

gāvau nau paramā mittā, yāsu jāyanti ausadhā||

299.

 ‘‘an‍nadā  baladā caitā, vaṇṇadā sukhadā tathā sukhadā ca tā (ka॰)|

aitamatthavasaṃ ñatvā, nāssu gāvau haniṃsu tai||

300.

 ‘‘sukhumālā  mahākāyā, vaṇṇavantau yasassinau|

brāhmaṇā saihi dhammaihi, kic‍cākic‍caisu ussukā|

yāva laukai avattiṃsu, sukhamaidhitthayaṃ pajā||

301.

 ‘‘taisaṃ āsi vipal‍lāsau, disvāna aṇutau aṇuṃ|

rājinau ca viyākāraṃ, nāriyau samalaṅkatā||

302.

 ‘‘rathai cājañ‍ñasaṃyuttai, sukatai cittasibbanai|

nivaisanai nivaisai ca, vibhattai bhāgasau mitai||

303.

 ‘‘gaumaṇḍalaparibyūḷhaṃ, nārīvaragaṇāyutaṃ|

uḷāraṃ mānusaṃ bhaugaṃ, abhijjhāyiṃsu brāhmaṇā||

304.

 ‘‘tai tattha mantai ganthaitvā, auk‍kākaṃ tadupāgamuṃ|

pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñausi, yajassu bahu tai vittaṃ|

yajassu bahu tai dhanaṃ||

305.

 ‘‘tatau ca rājā sañ‍ñattau, brāhmaṇaihi rathaisabhau|

assamaidhaṃ purisamaidhaṃ, sammāpāsaṃ vājapaiyyaṃ niraggaḷaṃ|

aitai  yāgai yajitvāna, brāhmaṇānamadā dhanaṃ||

306.

 ‘‘gāvau sayanañ‍ca vatthañ‍ca, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

rathai cājañ‍ñasaṃyuttai, sukatai cittasibbanai||

307.

 ‘‘nivaisanāni rammāni, suvibhattāni bhāgasau|

nānādhañ‍ñassa pūraitvā, brāhmaṇānamadā dhanaṃ||

308.

 ‘‘tai  ca tattha dhanaṃ laddhā, san‍nidhiṃ samaraucayuṃ|

taisaṃ icchāvatiṇṇānaṃ, bhiyyau taṇhā pavaḍḍhatha|

tai tattha mantai ganthaitvā, auk‍kākaṃ punamupāgamuṃ||

309.

 ‘‘yathā āpau ca pathavī ca, hirañ‍ñaṃ dhanadhāniyaṃ|

aivaṃ gāvau manussānaṃ, parikkhārau sau hi pāṇinaṃ|

yajassu bahu tai vittaṃ, yajassu bahu tai dhanaṃ||

310.

 ‘‘tatau ca rājā sañ‍ñattau, brāhmaṇaihi rathaisabhau|

naikā satasahassiyau, gāvau yañ‍ñai aghātayi||

311.

 ‘‘na  pādā na visāṇaina, nāssu hiṃsanti kainaci|

gāvau aiḷakasamānā, sauratā kumbhadūhanā|

tā visāṇai gahaitvāna, rājā satthaina ghātayi||

312.

 ‘‘tatau  daivā pitarau ca tatau ca daivā pitarau (sī॰ syā॰), indau asurarakkhasā|

adhammau iti pak‍kanduṃ, yaṃ satthaṃ nipatī gavai||

313.

 ‘‘tayau raugā purai āsuṃ, icchā anasanaṃ jarā|

pasūnañ‍ca samārambhā, aṭṭhānavutimāgamuṃ||

314.

 ‘‘aisau  adhammau daṇḍānaṃ, auk‍kantau purāṇau ahu|

adūsikāyau hañ‍ñanti, dhammā dhaṃsanti dhaṃsainti (sī॰ pī॰) yājakā||

315.

 ‘‘aivamaisau aṇudhammau, paurāṇau viñ‍ñugarahitau|

yattha aidisakaṃ passati, yājakaṃ garahatī garahī (ka॰) janau||

316.

 ‘‘aivaṃ dhammai viyāpan‍nai, vibhin‍nā suddavaissikā|

puthū vibhin‍nā khattiyā, patiṃ bhariyāvamañ‍ñatha||

317.

 ‘‘khattiyā  brahmabandhū ca, yai cañ‍ñai gauttarakkhitā|

jātivādaṃ niraṃkatvā nirākatvā (?) yathā anirākatajjhānauti, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagu’’nti||

aivaṃ vuttai, tai brāhmaṇamahāsālā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰ … upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

brāhmaṇadhammikasuttaṃ sattamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. nāvāsuttaṃ

318.

 yasmā  hi dhammaṃ purisau vijañ‍ñā, indaṃva naṃ daivatā pūjayaiyya|

sau pūjitau tasmi pasan‍nacittau, bahussutau pātukarauti dhammaṃ||

319.

 tadaṭṭhikatvāna  nisamma dhīrau, dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānau|

viñ‍ñū vibhāvī nipuṇau ca hauti, yau tādisaṃ bhajati appamattau||

320.

 khuddañ‍ca bālaṃ upasaivamānau, anāgatatthañ‍ca usūyakañ‍ca|

idhaiva dhammaṃ avibhāvayitvā, avitiṇṇakaṅkhau maraṇaṃ upaiti||

321.

 yathā narau āpagamautaritvā, mahaudakaṃ salilaṃ sīghasautaṃ|

sau vuyhamānau anusautagāmī, kiṃ sau parai sakkhati tārayaituṃ||

322.

 tathaiva dhammaṃ avibhāvayitvā, bahussutānaṃ anisāmayatthaṃ|

sayaṃ ajānaṃ avitiṇṇakaṅkhau, kiṃ  sau parai sakkhati nijjhapaituṃ||

323.

 yathāpi  nāvaṃ daḷhamāruhitvā, phiyaina piyaina (sī॰ syā॰) rittaina samaṅgibhūtau|

sau tārayai tattha bahūpi añ‍ñai, tatrūpayañ‍ñū kusalau mutīmā matīmā (syā॰ ka॰)||

324.

 aivampi  yau vaidagu bhāvitattau, bahussutau hauti avaidhadhammau|

sau khau parai nijjhapayai pajānaṃ, sautāvadhānūpanisūpapan‍nai||

325.

 tasmā havai sappurisaṃ bhajaitha, maidhāvinañ‍caiva bahussutañ‍ca|

añ‍ñāya atthaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānau, viñ‍ñātadhammau sa sukhaṃ sau sukhaṃ (sī॰) labhaithāti||

nāvāsuttaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. kiṃsīlasuttaṃ

326.

 ‘‘kiṃsīlau  kiṃsamācārau, kāni kammāni brūhayaṃ|

narau sammā niviṭṭhassa, uttamatthañ‍ca pāpuṇai’’||

327.

 ‘‘vuḍḍhāpacāyī anusūyakau siyā, kālañ‍ñū  kālañ‍ñu (sī॰ syā॰) cassa garūnaṃ garūnaṃ (sī॰) dassanāya|

dhammiṃ kathaṃ airayitaṃ khaṇañ‍ñū, suṇaiyya sak‍kac‍ca subhāsitāni||

328.

 ‘‘kālaina gacchai garūnaṃ sakāsaṃ, thambhaṃ niraṃkatvā nirākatvā (?) ni + ā + kara + tvā nivātavutti|

atthaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃyamaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, anussarai caiva samācarai ca||

329.

 ‘‘dhammārāmau dhammaratau, dhammai ṭhitau dhammavinicchayañ‍ñū|

naivācarai dhammasandausavādaṃ, tacchaihi nīyaitha subhāsitaihi||

330.

 ‘‘hassaṃ jappaṃ paridaivaṃ padausaṃ, māyākataṃ kuhanaṃ giddhi mānaṃ|

sārambhaṃ kak‍kasaṃ kasāvañ‍ca mucchaṃ sārambha kak‍kassa kasāva mucchaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), hitvā carai vītamadau ṭhitattau||

331.

 ‘‘viñ‍ñātasārāni subhāsitāni, sutañ‍ca viñ‍ñātasamādhisāraṃ|

na tassa pañ‍ñā ca sutañ‍ca vaḍḍhati, yau sāhasau hauti narau pamattau||

332.

 ‘‘dhammai  ca yai ariyapavaiditai ratā,

anuttarā  tai vacasā manasā kammunā ca|

tai santisaurac‍casamādhisaṇṭhitā,

sutassa pañ‍ñāya ca sāramajjhagū’’ti||

kiṃsīlasuttaṃ navamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. uṭṭhānasuttaṃ

333.

 uṭṭhahatha nisīdatha, kau atthau supitaina vau|

āturānañhi kā niddā, sal‍laviddhāna ruppataṃ||

334.

 uṭṭhahatha  nisīdatha, daḷhaṃ sikkhatha santiyā|

mā vau pamattai viñ‍ñāya, mac‍curājā amauhayittha vasānugai||

335.

 yāya daivā manussā ca, sitā tiṭṭhanti atthikā|

tarathaitaṃ visattikaṃ, khaṇau vau khaṇau vai (pī॰ ka॰) mā upac‍cagā|

khaṇātītā hi saucanti, nirayamhi samappitā||

336.

 pamādau rajau pamādau, pamādānupatitau rajau|

appamādaina vij‍jāya, abbahai abbūḷhai (syā॰ pī॰), abbuhai (ka॰ aṭṭha॰) sal‍lamattanauti||

uṭṭhānasuttaṃ dasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. rāhulasuttaṃ

337.

 ‘‘kac‍ci  abhiṇhasaṃvāsā, nāvajānāsi paṇḍitaṃ|

uk‍kādhārau auk‍kādhārau (syā॰ ka॰) manussānaṃ, kac‍ci apacitau tayā’’ tava (sī॰ aṭṭha॰)||

338.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ abhiṇhasaṃvāsā, avajānāmi paṇḍitaṃ|

uk‍kādhārau manussānaṃ, nic‍caṃ apacitau mayā’’||

339.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca  kāmaguṇai hitvā, piyarūpai manauramai|

saddhāya gharā nikkhamma, dukkhassantakarau bhava||

340.

 ‘‘mittai bhajassu kalyāṇai, pantañ‍ca sayanāsanaṃ|

vivittaṃ appanigghausaṃ, mattañ‍ñū hauhi bhaujanai||

341.

 ‘‘cīvarai  piṇḍapātai ca, pac‍cayai sayanāsanai|

aitaisu taṇhaṃ mākāsi, mā laukaṃ punarāgami||

342.

 ‘‘saṃvutau pātimaukkhasmiṃ, indriyaisu ca pañ‍casu|

sati kāyagatātyatthu, nibbidābahulau bhava||

343.

 ‘‘nimittaṃ parivaj‍jaihi, subhaṃ rāgūpasañhitaṃ|

asubhāya cittaṃ bhāvaihi, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

344.

 ‘‘animittañ‍ca bhāvaihi, mānānusayamuj‍jaha|

tatau mānābhisamayā, upasantau carissatī’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ bhagavā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ imāhi gāthāhi abhiṇhaṃ auvadatīti|

rāhulasuttaṃ aikādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. nigraudhakappasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa upajjhāyau nigraudhakappau nāma thairau aggāḷavai caitiyai aciraparinibbutau hauti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘parinibbutau nu khau mai upajjhāyau udāhu nau parinibbutau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘parinibbutau  nu khau mai upajjhāyau, udāhu nau parinabbutau’’’ti| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

345.

 ‘‘pucchāma pucchāmi (ka॰) satthāramanaumapañ‍ñaṃ, diṭṭhaiva dhammai yau vicikicchānaṃ chaittā|

aggāḷavai kālamakāsi bhikkhu, ñātau yasassī abhinibbutattau||

346.

 ‘‘nigraudhakappau  iti tassa nāmaṃ, tayā kataṃ bhagavā brāhmaṇassa|

sau taṃ namassaṃ acari mutyapaikkhau, āraddhavīriyau daḷhadhammadassī||

347.

 ‘‘taṃ sāvakaṃ sakya sak‍ka (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) mayampi sabbai, añ‍ñātumicchāma samantacakkhu|

samavaṭṭhitā nau savanāya sautā, tuvaṃ nau satthā tvamanuttarausi||

348.

 ‘‘chindaiva nau vicikicchaṃ brūhi maitaṃ, parinibbutaṃ vaidaya bhūripañ‍ña|

majjhaiva majjhai ca (syā॰ ka॰) nau bhāsa samantacakkhu, sak‍kauva daivāna sahassanaittau||

349.

 ‘‘yai kaici ganthā idha mauhamaggā, añ‍ñāṇapakkhā vicikicchaṭhānā|

tathāgataṃ  patvā na tai bhavanti, cakkhuñhi aitaṃ paramaṃ narānaṃ||

350.

 ‘‘nau  cai hi jātu purisau kilaisai, vātau yathā abbhadhanaṃ vihānai|

tamauvassa nivutau sabbalaukau, na  jautimantaupi narā tapaiyyuṃ||

351.

 ‘‘dhīrā  ca paj‍jautakarā bhavanti, taṃ taṃ ahaṃ vīra dhīra (sī॰ syā॰) tathaiva mañ‍ñai|

vipassinaṃ jānamupāgamumhā jānamupagamamhā (sī॰ syā॰), parisāsu nau āvikarauhi kappaṃ||

352.

 ‘‘khippaṃ giraṃ airaya vaggu vagguṃ, haṃsauva paggayha saṇikaṃ saṇiṃ (syā॰ pī॰) nikūja|

bindussaraina suvikappitaina, sabbaiva tai uj‍jugatā suṇauma||

353.

 ‘‘pahīnajātimaraṇaṃ asaisaṃ, niggayha dhaunaṃ dhautaṃ (sī॰) vadaissāmi dhammaṃ|

na kāmakārau hi puthuj‍janānaṃ, saṅkhaiyyakārau ca saṅkhayyakārauva (ka॰) tathāgatānaṃ||

354.

 ‘‘sampan‍navaiyyākaraṇaṃ tavaidaṃ, samuj‍jupañ‍ñassa samuj‍japañ‍ñassa (syā॰ ka॰) samuggahītaṃ|

ayamañ‍jalī pacchimau suppaṇāmitau, mā mauhayī jānamanaumapañ‍ña||

355.

 ‘‘parauvaraṃ varāvaraṃ (katthaci) ariyadhammaṃ viditvā, mā mauhayī jānamanaumavīra|

vāriṃ yathā  ghammani ghammatattau, vācābhikaṅkhāmi  sutaṃ pavassa sutassa vassa (syā॰)||

356.

 ‘‘yadatthikaṃ yadatthiyaṃ (pī॰), yadatthitaṃ (ka॰) brahmacariyaṃ acarī, kappāyanau kac‍cissa taṃ amaughaṃ|

nibbāyi sau ādu saupādisaisau, yathā vimuttau ahu taṃ suṇauma’’||

357.

 ‘‘acchaicchi  achaij‍ji (ka॰) taṇhaṃ idha nāmarūpai, (iti bhagavā)

kaṇhassa taṇhāya (ka॰) sautaṃ dīgharattānusayitaṃ|

atāri jātiṃ maraṇaṃ asaisaṃ,’’

ic‍cabravī bhagavā pañ‍casaiṭṭhau||

358.

 ‘‘aisa sutvā pasīdāmi, vacau tai isisattama|

amaughaṃ kira mai puṭṭhaṃ, na maṃ vañ‍caisi brāhmaṇau||

359.

 ‘‘yathāvādī  tathākārī, ahu buddhassa sāvakau|

acchidā mac‍cunau jālaṃ, tataṃ māyāvinau daḷhaṃ||

360.

 ‘‘addasā bhagavā ādiṃ, upādānassa kappiyau|

ac‍cagā vata kappāyanau, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttara’’nti||

nigraudhakappasuttaṃ dvādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

13. sammāparibbājanīyasuttaṃ

361.

 ‘‘pucchāmi  muniṃ pahūtapañ‍ñaṃ,

tiṇṇaṃ pāraṅgataṃ parinibbutaṃ ṭhitattaṃ|

nikkhamma gharā panuj‍ja kāmai, kathaṃ bhikkhu

sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya’’||

362.

 ‘‘yassa maṅgalā samūhatā, (iti bhagavā)

uppātā supinā ca lakkhaṇā ca|

sau maṅgaladausavippahīnau,

sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

363.

 ‘‘rāgaṃ vinayaitha mānusaisu, dibbaisu kāmaisu cāpi bhikkhu|

atik‍kamma bhavaṃ samaic‍ca dhammaṃ, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

364.

 ‘‘vipiṭṭhikatvāna  paisuṇāni, kaudhaṃ kadariyaṃ jahaiyya bhikkhu|

anuraudhaviraudhavippahīnau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

365.

 ‘‘hitvāna  piyañ‍ca appiyañ‍ca, anupādāya anissitau kuhiñ‍ci|

saṃyaujaniyaihi vippamuttau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

366.

 ‘‘na  sau upadhīsu sāramaiti, ādānaisu vinaiyya chandarāgaṃ|

sau anissitau anañ‍ñanaiyyau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

367.

 ‘‘vacasā  manasā ca kammunā ca, aviruddhau sammā viditvā dhammaṃ|

nibbānapadābhipatthayānau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

368.

 ‘‘yau vandati manti nuṇṇamaiyya nun‍namaiyya (?), ak‍kuṭṭhaupi na sandhiyaitha bhikkhu|

laddhā parabhaujanaṃ na maj‍jai, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

369.

 ‘‘laubhañ‍ca bhavañ‍ca vippahāya, viratau chaidanabandhanā ca chaidanabandhanatau (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhu|

sau tiṇṇakathaṃkathau visal‍lau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

370.

 ‘‘sāruppaṃ  attanau viditvā, nau ca bhikkhu hiṃsaiyya kañ‍ci laukai|

yathā tathiyaṃ viditvā dhammaṃ, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

371.

 ‘‘yassānusayā  na santi kaici, mūlā ca mūlā (sī॰ syā॰) akusalā samūhatāsai|

sau nirāsau nirāsayau (sī॰), nirāsasau (syā॰) anāsisānau anāsayānau (sī॰ pī॰), anāsasānau (syā॰), sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

372.

 ‘‘āsavakhīṇau  pahīnamānau, sabbaṃ rāgapathaṃ upātivattau|

dantau parinibbutau ṭhitattau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

373.

 ‘‘saddhau sutavā niyāmadassī, vaggagataisu na vaggasāri dhīrau|

laubhaṃ dausaṃ vinaiyya paṭighaṃ, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

374.

 ‘‘saṃsuddhajinau vivaṭṭacchadau, dhammaisu vasī pāragū anaijau|

saṅkhāraniraudhañāṇakusalau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

375.

 ‘‘atītaisu anāgataisu cāpi, kappātītau atic‍casuddhipañ‍ñau|

sabbāyatanaihi vippamuttau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

376.

 ‘‘añ‍ñāya  padaṃ samaic‍ca dhammaṃ, vivaṭaṃ disvāna pahānamāsavānaṃ|

sabbupadhīnaṃ parikkhayānau parikkhayā (pī॰), sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya’’||

377.

 ‘‘addhā  hi bhagavā tathaiva aitaṃ, yau sau aivaṃvihārī dantau bhikkhu|

sabbasaṃyaujanayaugavītivattau  sabbasaṃyaujaniyai ca vītivattau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyyā’’ti||

sammāparibbājanīyasuttaṃ tairasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

14. dhammikasuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau dhammikau upāsakau  pañ‍cahi upāsakasataihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dhammikau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

378.

 ‘‘pucchāmi taṃ gautama bhūripañ‍ña, kathaṃkarau sāvakau sādhu hauti|

yau vā agārā anagāramaiti, agārinau vā panupāsakāsai||

379.

 ‘‘tuvañhi laukassa sadaivakassa, gatiṃ pajānāsi parāyaṇañ‍ca|

na catthi tulyau nipuṇatthadassī, tuvañhi buddhaṃ pavaraṃ vadanti||

380.

 ‘‘sabbaṃ tuvaṃ ñāṇamavaic‍ca dhammaṃ, pakāsaisi sattai anukampamānau|

vivaṭṭacchadausi samantacakkhu, viraucasi vimalau sabbalaukai||

381.

 ‘‘āgañchi  tai santikai nāgarājā, airāvaṇau nāma jinauti sutvā|

saupi tayā mantayitvājjhagamā, sādhūti sutvāna patītarūpau||

382.

 ‘‘rājāpi  taṃ vaissavaṇau kuvairau, upaiti dhammaṃ paripucchamānau|

tassāpi tvaṃ pucchitau brūsi dhīra, sau cāpi sutvāna patītarūpau||

383.

 ‘‘yai kaicimai titthiyā vādasīlā, ājīvakā vā yadi vā nigaṇṭhā|

pañ‍ñāya taṃ nātitaranti sabbai, ṭhitau vajantaṃ viya sīghagāmiṃ||

384.

 ‘‘yai kaicimai brāhmaṇā vādasīlā, vuddhā cāpi brāhmaṇā santi kaici|

sabbai tayi atthabaddhā bhavanti, yai cāpi añ‍ñai vādinau mañ‍ñamānā||

385.

 ‘‘ayañhi dhammau nipuṇau sukhau ca, yauyaṃ tayā bhagavā suppavuttau|

tamaiva sabbaipi sabbai mayaṃ (syā॰) sussūsamānā, taṃ nau vada pucchitau buddhasaiṭṭha||

386.

 ‘‘sabbaipi mai bhikkhavau san‍nisin‍nā, upāsakā cāpi tathaiva sautuṃ|

suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ, subhāsitaṃ  vāsavassaiva daivā’’||

387.

 ‘‘suṇātha mai bhikkhavau sāvayāmi vau, dhammaṃ dhutaṃ tañ‍ca carātha sabbai|

iriyāpathaṃ  pabbajitānulaumikaṃ, saivaitha naṃ atthadasau mutīmā||

388.

 ‘‘nau vai vikālai vicaraiyya bhikkhu, gāmai ca piṇḍāya caraiyya kālai|

akālacāriñhi sajanti saṅgā, tasmā vikālai na caranti buddhā||

389.

 ‘‘rūpā  ca saddā ca rasā ca gandhā, phassā ca yai sammadayanti sattai|

aitaisu dhammaisu vinaiyya chandaṃ, kālaina sau pavisai pātarāsaṃ||

390.

 ‘‘piṇḍañ‍ca bhikkhu samayaina laddhā, aikau paṭik‍kamma rahau nisīdai|

ajjhattacintī na manau bahiddhā, nicchārayai saṅgahitattabhāvau||

391.

 ‘‘sacaipi sau sal‍lapai sāvakaina, añ‍ñaina vā kainaci bhikkhunā vā|

dhammaṃ  paṇītaṃ tamudāharaiyya, na paisuṇaṃ naupi parūpavādaṃ||

392.

 ‘‘vādañhi  aikai paṭisainiyanti, na tai pasaṃsāma parittapañ‍ñai|

tatau tatau nai pasajanti saṅgā, cittañhi tai tattha gamainti dūrai||

393.

 ‘‘piṇḍaṃ vihāraṃ sayanāsanañ‍ca, āpañ‍ca saṅghāṭirajūpavāhanaṃ|

sutvāna dhammaṃ sugataina daisitaṃ, saṅkhāya saivai varapañ‍ñasāvakau||

394.

 ‘‘tasmā hi piṇḍai sayanāsanai ca, āpai ca saṅghāṭirajūpavāhanai|

aitaisu  dhammaisu anūpalittau, bhikkhu yathā paukkharai vāribindu||

395.

 ‘‘gahaṭṭhavattaṃ pana vau vadāmi, yathākarau sāvakau sādhu hauti|

na haisa na haisau (sī॰) labbhā sapariggahaina, phassaituṃ yau kaivalau bhikkhudhammau||

396.

 ‘‘pāṇaṃ  na hanai na hānai (sī॰) na ca ghātayaiyya, na cānujañ‍ñā hanataṃ paraisaṃ|

sabbaisu  bhūtaisu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ, yai thāvarā yai ca tasā santi tasanti (sī॰ pī॰) laukai||

397.

 ‘‘tatau adin‍naṃ parivaj‍jayaiyya, kiñ‍ci k‍vaci sāvakau bujjhamānau|

na hārayai harataṃ nānujañ‍ñā, sabbaṃ adin‍naṃ parivaj‍jayaiyya||

398.

 ‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ parivaj‍jayaiyya, aṅgārakāsuṃ jalitaṃva viñ‍ñū|

asambhuṇantau pana brahmacariyaṃ, parassa dāraṃ na atik‍kamaiyya||

399.

 ‘‘sabhaggatau vā parisaggatau vā, aikassa vaikau caitau (sī॰ syā॰) na musā bhaṇaiyya|

na bhāṇayai bhaṇataṃ nānujañ‍ñā, sabbaṃ abhūtaṃ parivaj‍jayaiyya||

400.

 ‘‘maj‍jañ‍ca pānaṃ na samācaraiyya, dhammaṃ imaṃ raucayai yau gahaṭṭhau|

na pāyayai pivataṃ nānujañ‍ñā, ummādanantaṃ iti naṃ viditvā||

401.

 ‘‘madā hi pāpāni karaunti bālā, kārainti cañ‍ñaipi janai pamattai|

aitaṃ  apuñ‍ñāyatanaṃ vivaj‍jayai, ummādanaṃ mauhanaṃ bālakantaṃ||

402.

 ‘‘pāṇaṃ  na hanai na cādin‍namādiyai, musā na bhāsai na ca maj‍japau siyā|

abrahmacariyā viramaiyya maithunā, rattiṃ na bhuñ‍jaiyya vikālabhaujanaṃ||

403.

 ‘‘mālaṃ  na dhārai na ca gandhamācarai, mañ‍cai chamāyaṃ va sayaitha santhatai|

aitañhi aṭṭhaṅgikamāhupausathaṃ, buddhaina dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ||

404.

 ‘‘tatau ca pakkhassupavassupausathaṃ, cātuddasiṃ pañ‍cadasiñ‍ca aṭṭhamiṃ|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca pasan‍namānasau, aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ susamattarūpaṃ||

405.

 ‘‘tatau ca pātau upavutthupausathau, an‍naina pānaina ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau anumaudamānau, yathārahaṃ saṃvibhajaitha viñ‍ñū||

406.

 ‘‘dhammaina mātāpitarau bharaiyya, payaujayai dhammikaṃ sau vaṇij‍jaṃ|

aitaṃ  gihī vattayamappamattau, sayampabhai nāma upaiti daivai’’ti||

dhammikasuttaṃ cuddasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

cūḷavaggau dutiyau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

ratanāmagandhau hiri ca, maṅgalaṃ sūcilaumaina|

dhammacariyañ‍ca brāhmaṇau kapilau brāhmaṇaupi ca (syā॰ ka॰), nāvā kiṃsīlamuṭṭhānaṃ||

rāhulau puna kappau ca, paribbājaniyaṃ tathā|

dhammikañ‍ca vidunau āhu, cūḷavagganti cuddasāti||


3. mahāvaggau

1. pabbaj‍jāsuttaṃ

407.

 pabbaj‍jaṃ  kittayissāmi, yathā pabbaji cakkhumā|

yathā vīmaṃsamānau sau, pabbaj‍jaṃ samaraucayi||

408.

 sambādhauyaṃ gharāvāsau, rajassāyatanaṃ iti|

abbhaukāsauva pabbaj‍jā, iti disvāna pabbaji||

409.

 pabbajitvāna kāyaina, pāpakammaṃ vivaj‍jayi|

vacīduc‍caritaṃ  hitvā, ājīvaṃ parisaudhayi||

410.

 agamā rājagahaṃ buddhau, magadhānaṃ giribbajaṃ|

piṇḍāya abhihāraisi, ākiṇṇavaralakkhaṇau||

411.

 tamaddasā bimbisārau, pāsādasmiṃ patiṭṭhitau|

disvā lakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ, imamatthaṃ abhāsatha||

412.

 ‘‘imaṃ bhauntau nisāmaitha, abhirūpau brahā suci|

caraṇaina ca sampan‍nau, yugamattañ‍ca paikkhati||

413.

 ‘‘aukkhittacakkhu satimā, nāyaṃ nīcakulāmiva|

rājadūtābhidhāvantu, kuhiṃ bhikkhu gamissati’’||

414.

 tai paisitā rājadūtā, piṭṭhitau anubandhisuṃ|

kuhiṃ gamissati bhikkhu, kattha vāsau bhavissati||

415.

 sapadānaṃ  caramānau, guttadvārau susaṃvutau|

khippaṃ pattaṃ apūraisi, sampajānau paṭissatau||

416.

 piṇḍacāraṃ  caritvāna, nikkhamma nagarā muni|

paṇḍavaṃ abhihāraisi, aittha vāsau bhavissati||

417.

 disvāna vāsūpagataṃ, tayau tatau (sī॰ pī॰) dūtā upāvisuṃ|

taisu aikauva aikau ca dūtau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) āgantvā, rājinau paṭivaidayi||

418.

 ‘‘aisa  bhikkhu mahārāja, paṇḍavassa puratthatau purakkhatau (syā॰ ka॰)|

nisin‍nau byagghusabhauva, sīhauva girigabbharai’’||

419.

 sutvāna  dūtavacanaṃ, bhaddayānaina khattiyau|

taramānarūpau niyyāsi, yaina paṇḍavapabbatau||

420.

 sa yānabhūmiṃ yāyitvā, yānā auruyha khattiyau|

pattikau upasaṅkamma, āsaj‍ja naṃ upāvisi||

421.

 nisaj‍ja rājā sammaudi, kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ tatau|

kathaṃ sau vītisāraitvā, imamatthaṃ abhāsatha||

422.

 ‘‘yuvā ca daharau cāsi, paṭhamuppattikau paṭhamuppattiyā (sī॰), paṭhamuppattitau (syā॰) susu|

vaṇṇārauhaina sampan‍nau, jātimā viya khattiyau||

423.

 ‘‘saubhayantau anīkaggaṃ, nāgasaṅghapurakkhatau|

dadāmi bhaugai bhuñ‍jassu, jātiṃ akkhāhi pucchitau’’||

424.

 ‘‘ujuṃ janapadau rāja, himavantassa passatau|

dhanavīriyaina sampan‍nau, kausalaisu kausalassa (syā॰ ka॰) nikaitinau||

425.

 ‘‘ādic‍cā  ādic‍cau (ka॰) nāma gauttaina, sākiyā sākiyau (ka॰) nāma jātiyā|

tamhā kulā pabbajitaumhi, na kāmai abhipatthayaṃ||

426.

 ‘‘kāmaisvādīnavaṃ disvā, naikkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khaimatau|

padhānāya gamissāmi, aittha mai rañ‍jatī manau’’ti||

pabbaj‍jāsuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. padhānasuttaṃ

427.

 ‘‘taṃ  maṃ padhānapahitattaṃ, nadiṃ nairañ‍jaraṃ pati|

viparak‍kamma jhāyantaṃ, yaugakkhaimassa pattiyā||

428.

 ‘‘namucī karuṇaṃ vācaṃ, bhāsamānau upāgami|

‘kisau tvamasi dubbaṇṇau, santikai maraṇaṃ tava||

429.

 ‘‘‘sahassabhāgau  maraṇassa, aikaṃsau tava jīvitaṃ|

jīva bhau jīvitaṃ saiyyau, jīvaṃ puñ‍ñāni kāhasi||

430.

 ‘‘‘caratau  ca tai brahmacariyaṃ, aggihuttañ‍ca jūhatau|

pahūtaṃ cīyatai puñ‍ñaṃ, kiṃ padhānaina kāhasi||

431.

 ‘‘‘duggau maggau padhānāya, duk‍karau durabhisambhavau’’’|

imā gāthā bhaṇaṃ mārau, aṭṭhā buddhassa santikai||

432.

 taṃ tathāvādinaṃ māraṃ, bhagavā aitadabravi|

‘‘pamattabandhu pāpima, yainatthaina sainatthaina (?), attanau atthaina (aṭṭha॰ saṃvaṇṇanā) idhāgatau||

433.

 ‘‘aṇumattaupi  aṇumattainapi (sī॰ syā॰) puñ‍ñaina, atthau mayhaṃ na vij‍jati|

yaisañ‍ca atthau puñ‍ñaina, tai mārau vattumarahati||

434.

 ‘‘atthi saddhā tathā tatau (sī॰ pī॰), tapau (syā॰ ka॰) vīriyaṃ, pañ‍ñā ca mama vij‍jati|

aivaṃ maṃ pahitattampi, kiṃ jīvamanupucchasi||

435.

 ‘‘nadīnamapi sautāni, ayaṃ vātau visausayai|

kiñ‍ca  mai pahitattassa, lauhitaṃ nupasussayai||

436.

 ‘‘lauhitai sussamānamhi, pittaṃ saimhañ‍ca sussati|

maṃsaisu khīyamānaisu, bhiyyau cittaṃ pasīdati|

bhiyyau sati ca pañ‍ñā ca, samādhi mama tiṭṭhati||

437.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, pattassuttamavaidanaṃ|

kāmaisu kāmai (sī॰ syā॰) nāpaikkhatai cittaṃ, passa sattassa suddhataṃ||

438.

 ‘‘kāmā  tai paṭhamā sainā, dutiyā arati vuc‍cati|

tatiyā khuppipāsā tai, catutthī taṇhā pavuc‍cati||

439.

 ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ pañ‍camī (sī॰ pī॰) thinamiddhaṃ tai, chaṭṭhā bhīrū pavuc‍cati|

sattamī vicikicchā tai, makkhau thambhau tai aṭṭhamau||

440.

 ‘‘lābhau silaukau sak‍kārau, micchāladdhau ca yau yasau|

yau cattānaṃ samuk‍kaṃsai, parai ca avajānati||

441.

 ‘‘aisā  namuci tai sainā, kaṇhassābhippahārinī|

na naṃ asūrau jināti, jaitvā ca labhatai sukhaṃ||

442.

 ‘‘aisa muñ‍jaṃ pariharai, dhiratthu mama ida (ka॰) jīvitaṃ|

saṅgāmai mai mataṃ saiyyau, yaṃ cai jīvai parājitau||

443.

 ‘‘pagāḷhaittha  na dissanti, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

tañ‍ca maggaṃ na jānanti, yaina gacchanti subbatā||

444.

 ‘‘samantā dhajiniṃ disvā, yuttaṃ māraṃ savāhanaṃ|

yuddhāya pac‍cuggacchāmi, mā maṃ ṭhānā acāvayi||

445.

 ‘‘yaṃ  tai taṃ nappasahati, sainaṃ laukau sadaivakau|

taṃ  tai pañ‍ñāya bhaicchāmi gacchāmi (sī॰), vaicchāmi (syā॰), vajjhāmi (ka॰), āmaṃ pattaṃva asmanā pak‍kaṃva amunā (ka॰)||

446.

 ‘‘vasīkaritvā vasiṃ karitvā (bahūsu) saṅkappaṃ, satiñ‍ca sūpatiṭṭhitaṃ|

raṭṭhā raṭṭhaṃ vicarissaṃ, sāvakai vinayaṃ puthū||

447.

 ‘‘tai appamattā pahitattā, mama sāsanakārakā|

akāmassa akāmā (ka॰) tai gamissanti, yattha gantvā na saucarai’’||

448.

 ‘‘satta vassāni bhagavantaṃ, anubandhiṃ padāpadaṃ|

autāraṃ nādhigacchissaṃ, sambuddhassa satīmatau||

449.

 ‘‘maidavaṇṇaṃva pāsāṇaṃ, vāyasau anupariyagā|

apaittha muduṃ mudu (sī॰) vindaima, api assādanā siyā||

450.

 ‘‘aladdhā tattha assādaṃ, vāyasaittau apak‍kami|

kākauva sailamāsaj‍ja, nibbij‍jāpaima gautamaṃ’’||

451.

 tassa  saukaparaitassa, vīṇā kacchā abhassatha|

tatau sau dummanau yakkhau, tatthaivantaradhāyathāti||

padhānasuttaṃ dutiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. subhāsitasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aika samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā vācā subhāsitā hauti, na dubbhāsitā, anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu subhāsitaṃyaiva bhāsati nau dubbhāsitaṃ, dhammaṃyaiva bhāsati nau adhammaṃ, piyaṃyaiva bhāsati nau appiyaṃ, sac‍caṃyaiva bhāsati nau alikaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā vācā subhāsitā hauti, nau dubbhāsitā, anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūna’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

452.

 ‘‘subhāsitaṃ uttamamāhu santau, dhammaṃ bhaṇai nādhammaṃ taṃ dutiyaṃ|

piyaṃ bhaṇai nāppiyaṃ taṃ tatiyaṃ, sac‍caṃ bhaṇai nālikaṃ taṃ catuttha’’nti||

atha  khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

453.

 ‘‘tamaiva vācaṃ bhāsaiyya, yāyattānaṃ na tāpayai|

parai  ca na vihiṃsaiyya, sā vai vācā subhāsitā||

454.

 ‘‘piyavācamaiva bhāsaiyya, yā vācā paṭinanditā|

yaṃ anādāya pāpāni, paraisaṃ bhāsatai piyaṃ||

455.

 ‘‘sac‍caṃ vai amatā vācā, aisa dhammau sanantanau|

sac‍cai atthai ca dhammai ca, āhu santau patiṭṭhitā||

456.

 ‘‘yaṃ  buddhau bhāsati vācaṃ, khaimaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

dukkhassantakiriyāya, sā vai vācānamuttamā’’ti||

subhāsitasuttaṃ tatiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. sundarikabhāradvājasuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati sundarikāya nadiyā tīrai| taina khau pana samayaina sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau sundarikāya nadiyā tīrai aggiṃ juhati, aggihuttaṃ paricarati| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau aggiṃ juhitvā aggihuttaṃ paricaritvā uṭṭhāyāsanā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaisi – ‘‘kau nu khau imaṃ habyasaisaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyyā’’ti? addasā khau sundarikabhāradvājau  brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai sasīsaṃ pārutaṃ nisin‍naṃ; disvāna vāmaina hatthaina habyasaisaṃ gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina kamaṇḍaluṃ gahaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami|

atha khau bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa padasaddaina sīsaṃ  vivari| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘muṇḍau ayaṃ bhavaṃ, muṇḍakau ayaṃ bhava’’nti tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘muṇḍāpi hi idhaikac‍cai brāhmaṇā bhavanti, yaṃnūnāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā jātiṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃjac‍cau bhava’’nti?

atha khau bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

457.

 ‘‘na brāhmaṇau naumhi na rājaputtau, na vaissāyanau uda kauci naumhi|

gauttaṃ pariñ‍ñāya puthuj‍janānaṃ, akiñ‍canau manta carāmi laukai||

458.

 ‘‘saṅghāṭivāsī  agahau carāmi agihau (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) agaihau (katthaci), nivuttakaisau abhinibbutattau|

alippamānau idha māṇavaihi, akal‍laṃ maṃ brāhmaṇa pucchasi gauttapañhaṃ’’||

459.

 ‘‘pucchanti  vai bhau brāhmaṇā, brāhmaṇaibhi saha brāhmaṇau nau bhava’’nti||

460.

 ‘‘brāhmaṇau hi cai tvaṃ brūsi, mañ‍ca brūsi abrāhmaṇaṃ|

taṃ taṃ sāvittiṃ pucchāmi, tipadaṃ catuvīsatakkharaṃ||

461.

 ‘‘kiṃ  nissitā isayau manujā, khattiyā brāhmaṇā paṭhamapādantau daivatānaṃ|

yañ‍ñamakappayiṃsu puthū idha laukai dutiyapādantau (sī॰)||

462.

 ‘‘yadantagū vaidagū yañ‍ñakālai, yassāhutiṃ labhai tassijjhaiti brūmi’’||

463.

 ‘‘addhā hi tassa hutamijjhai, (iti brāhmaṇau)

yaṃ tādisaṃ vaidagumaddasāma|
