tumhādisānañhi adassanaina, añ‍ñau janau bhuñ‍jati pūraḷāsaṃ’’||

464.

 ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ brāhmaṇa atthaina, atthikau upasaṅkamma puccha|

santaṃ vidhūmaṃ anīghaṃ nirāsaṃ, appaividha abhivindai sumaidhaṃ’’||

465.

 ‘‘yañ‍ñai ratauhaṃ bhau gautama, yañ‍ñaṃ yiṭṭhukāmau nāhaṃ pajānāmi|

anusāsatu maṃ bhavaṃ, yattha  hutaṃ ijjhatai brūhi mai taṃ’’||

‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, audahassu sautaṃ; dhammaṃ tai daisaissāmi –

466.

 ‘‘mā  jātiṃ pucchī caraṇañ‍ca puccha, kaṭṭhā havai jāyati jātavaidau|

nīcākulīnaupi  munī dhitīmā, ājāniyau hauti hirīnisaidhau||

467.

 ‘‘sac‍caina dantau damasā upaitau, vaidantagū vūsitabrahmacariyau|

kālaina tamhi habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau puñ‍ñapaikhau (sī॰ pī॰) yajaitha||

468.

 ‘‘yai  kāmai hitvā agahā caranti, susañ‍ñatattā tasaraṃva uj‍juṃ|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

469.

 ‘‘yai vītarāgā susamāhitindriyā, candauva rāhuggahaṇā pamuttā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

470.

 ‘‘asaj‍jamānā vicaranti laukai, sadā satā hitvā mamāyitāni|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

471.

 ‘‘yau kāmai hitvā abhibhuyyacārī, yau vaidi jātīmaraṇassa antaṃ|

parinibbutau  udakarahadauva sītau, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

472.

 ‘‘samau  samaihi visamaihi dūrai, tathāgatau hauti anantapañ‍ñau|

anūpalittau idha vā huraṃ vā, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

473.

 ‘‘yamhi  na māyā vasati na mānau, yau vītalaubhau amamau nirāsau|

panuṇṇakaudhau abhinibbutattau, yau brāhmaṇau saukamalaṃ ahāsi|

tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

474 .

‘‘nivaisanaṃ yau manasau ahāsi, pariggahā yassa na santi kaici|

anupādiyānau idha vā huraṃ vā, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

475.

 ‘‘samāhitau yau udatāri aughaṃ, dhammaṃ cañ‍ñāsi paramāya diṭṭhiyā|

khīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

476.

 ‘‘bhavāsavā yassa vacī kharā ca, vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi|

sa vaidagū sabbadhi vippamuttau, tathāgatau  arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

477.

 ‘‘saṅgātigau yassa na santi saṅgā, yau mānasattaisu amānasattau|

dukkhaṃ  pariñ‍ñāya sakhaittavatthuṃ, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

478.

 ‘‘āsaṃ  anissāya vivaikadassī, paravaidiyaṃ diṭṭhimupātivattau|

ārammaṇā yassa na santi kaici, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

479.

 ‘‘parauparā parauvarā (sī॰ pī॰) yassa samaic‍ca dhammā, vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi|

santau upādānakhayai vimuttau, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

480.

 ‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ  jātikhayantadassī, yaupānudi rāgapathaṃ asaisaṃ|

suddhau nidausau vimalau akācau akāmau (sī॰ syā॰), tathāgatau  arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

481.

 ‘‘yau attanau attānaṃ attanāttānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) nānupassati, samāhitau uj‍jugatau ṭhitattau|

sa vai anaijau akhilau akaṅkhau, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ||

482.

 ‘‘mauhantarā yassa na santi kaici, sabbaisu dhammaisu ca ñāṇadassī|

sarīrañ‍ca antimaṃ dhāraiti, pattau ca sambaudhimanuttaraṃ sivaṃ|

aittāvatā yakkhassa suddhi, tathāgatau arahati pūraḷāsaṃ’’||

483.

 ‘‘hutañ‍ca  huttañ‍ca (sī॰ ka॰) mayhaṃ hutamatthu sac‍caṃ, yaṃ tādisaṃ vaidagunaṃ alatthaṃ|

brahmā hi sakkhi paṭigaṇhātu mai bhagavā, bhuñ‍jatu mai bhagavā pūraḷāsaṃ’’||

484.

 ‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyyaṃ, sampassataṃ brāhmaṇa naisa dhammau|

gāthābhigītaṃ panudanti buddhā, dhammai satī brāhmaṇa vuttiraisā||

485.

 ‘‘añ‍ñaina ca kaivalinaṃ mahaisiṃ, khīṇāsavaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cavūpasantaṃ|

an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhahassu, khaittañhi taṃ puñ‍ñapaikkhassa hauti’’||

486.

 ‘‘sādhāhaṃ  bhagavā tathā vijañ‍ñaṃ, yau dakkhiṇaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya mādisassa|

yaṃ yañ‍ñakālai pariyaisamānau, pappuyya tava sāsanaṃ’’||

487.

 ‘‘sārambhā yassa vigatā, cittaṃ yassa anāvilaṃ|

vippamuttau ca kāmaihi, thinaṃ yassa panūditaṃ||

488.

 ‘‘sīmantānaṃ  vinaitāraṃ, jātimaraṇakauvidaṃ|

muniṃ maunaiyyasampan‍naṃ, tādisaṃ yañ‍ñamāgataṃ||

489.

 ‘‘bhakuṭiṃ bhūkuṭiṃ (ka॰ sī॰), bhākuṭiṃ (ka॰ sī॰, ma॰ ni॰ 1.226) vinayitvāna, pañ‍jalikā namassatha|

pūjaitha an‍napānaina, aivaṃ ijjhanti dakkhiṇā||

490.

 ‘‘buddhau  bhavaṃ arahati pūraḷāsaṃ, puñ‍ñakhaittamanuttaraṃ|

āyāgau sabbalaukassa, bhautau din‍naṃ mahapphala’’nti||

atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| alattha khau  sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau…pai॰… arahataṃ ahausīti|

sundarikabhāradvājasuttaṃ catutthaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. māghasuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aika samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau māghau māṇavau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau māghau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca –

‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, dāyakau dānapati vadañ‍ñū yācayaugau; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisāmi; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā dhammaladdhaihi bhaugaihi dhammādhigataihi aikassapi dadāmi dvin‍nampi  tiṇṇampi catun‍nampi pañ‍can‍nampi chan‍nampi sattan‍nampi aṭṭhan‍nampi navan‍nampi dasan‍nampi dadāmi, vīsāyapi tiṃsāyapi cattālīsāyapi pañ‍ñāsāyapi dadāmi, satassapi dadāmi, bhiyyaupi dadāmi| kac‍cāhaṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃ dadantau aivaṃ yajantau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavāmī’’ti?

‘‘taggha tvaṃ, māṇava, aivaṃ dadantau aivaṃ yajantau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| yau khau, māṇava, dāyakau dānapati vadañ‍ñū yācayaugau; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati; dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā dhammaladdhaihi bhaugaihi dhammādhigataihi aikassapi dadāti…pai॰… satassapi dadāti, bhiyyaupi dadāti, bahuṃ sau puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| atha khau māghau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

491.

 ‘‘pucchāmahaṃ gautamaṃ vadañ‍ñuṃ, (iti māghau māṇavau)

kāsāyavāsiṃ agahaṃ agihaṃ (sī॰), agaihaṃ (pī॰) carantaṃ|

yau yācayaugau dānapati dānapatī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gahaṭṭhau, puñ‍ñatthikau puñ‍ñapaikhau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) yajati puñ‍ñapaikkhau|

dadaṃ  paraisaṃ idha an‍napānaṃ, kathaṃ hutaṃ yajamānassa sujjhai’’||

492.

 ‘‘yau yācayaugau dānapati gahaṭṭhau, (māghāti bhagavā)

puñ‍ñatthikau yajati puñ‍ñapaikkhau|

dadaṃ paraisaṃ idha an‍napānaṃ, ārādhayai dakkhiṇaiyyaibhi tādi’’||

493.

 ‘‘yau  yācayaugau dānapati gahaṭṭhau, (iti māghau māṇavau)

puñ‍ñatthikau yajati puñ‍ñapaikkhau|

dadaṃ paraisaṃ idha an‍napānaṃ, akkhāhi mai bhagavā dakkhiṇaiyyai’’||

494.

 ‘‘yai  vai asattā alaggā (syā॰) vicaranti laukai, akiñ‍canā kaivalinau yatattā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

495.

 ‘‘yai sabbasaṃyaujanabandhanacchidā, dantā vimuttā anīghā nirāsā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

496.

 ‘‘yai  sabbasaṃyaujanavippamuttā, dantā vimuttā anīghā nirāsā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

497.

 ‘‘rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ, khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

498.

 ‘‘yaisu na māyā vasati na mānau, khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

499.

 ‘‘yai  vītalaubhā amamā nirāsā, khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

500.

 ‘‘yai  vai na taṇhāsu upātipan‍nā, vitaraiyya aughaṃ amamā caranti|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

501.

 ‘‘yaisaṃ taṇhā natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai, bhavābhavāya idha vā huraṃ vā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

502.

 ‘‘yai kāmai hitvā agahā caranti, susañ‍ñatattā tasaraṃva uj‍juṃ|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

503.

 ‘‘yai vītarāgā susamāhitindriyā, candauva rāhuggahaṇā pamuttā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

504.

 ‘‘samitāvinau vītarāgā akaupā, yaisaṃ gatī natthidha vippahāya|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

505.

 ‘‘jahitvā jātimaraṇaṃ asaisaṃ, kathaṃkathiṃ sabbamupātivattā|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

506.

 ‘‘yai  attadīpā vicaranti laukai, akiñ‍canā sabbadhi vippamuttā|

kālaina  taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

507.

 ‘‘yai haittha jānanti yathā tathā idaṃ, ayamantimā natthi punabbhavauti|

kālaina taisu habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha||

508.

 ‘‘yau  vaidagū jhānaratau satīmā, sambaudhipattau saraṇaṃ bahūnaṃ|

kālaina tamhi habyaṃ pavaicchai, yau brāhmaṇau puñ‍ñapaikkhau yajaitha’’||

509.

 ‘‘addhā amaughā mama pucchanā ahu, akkhāsi mai bhagavā dakkhiṇaiyyai|

tvañhaittha jānāsi yathā tathā idaṃ, tathā hi tai viditau aisa dhammau||

510.

 ‘‘yau yācayaugau dānapati gahaṭṭhau, (iti māghau māṇavau)

puñ‍ñatthikau yajati puñ‍ñapaikkhau|

dadaṃ paraisaṃ idha an‍napānaṃ,

akkhāhi mai bhagavā yañ‍ñasampadaṃ’’||

511.

 ‘‘yajassu yajamānau māghāti bhagavā, sabbattha ca vippasādaihi cittaṃ|

ārammaṇaṃ yajamānassa yañ‍ñau, aitthappatiṭṭhāya jahāti dausaṃ||

512.

 ‘‘sau  vītarāgau pavinaiyya dausaṃ, maittaṃ cittaṃ bhāvayamappamāṇaṃ|

rattindivaṃ satatamappamattau, sabbā disā pharati appamañ‍ñaṃ’’||

513.

 ‘‘kau  sujjhati muc‍cati bajjhatī ca, kainattanā gacchati kainatthainā gacchati (ka॰) brahmalaukaṃ|

ajānatau mai muni brūhi puṭṭhau, bhagavā hi mai sakkhi brahmaj‍jadiṭṭhau|

tuvañhi  nau brahmasamausi sac‍caṃ, kathaṃ upapaj‍jati brahmalaukaṃ jutima’’||

514.

 ‘‘yau yajati tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ, (māghāti bhagavā)

ārādhayai dakkhiṇaiyyaibhi tādi|

aivaṃ  yajitvā sammā yācayaugau,

upapaj‍jati brahmalaukanti brūmī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, māghau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

māghasuttaṃ pañ‍camaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. sabhiyasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina sabhiyassa paribbājakassa purāṇasālauhitāya daivatāya pañhā uddiṭṭhā haunti – ‘‘yau tai, sabhiya, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imai pañhai puṭṭhau byākarauti tassa santikai brahmacariyaṃ caraiyyāsī’’ti|

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau tassā daivatāya santikai tai pañhai uggahaitvā yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā  bahujanassa, saiyyathidaṃ – pūraṇau kassapau makkhaligausālau ajitau kaisakambalau pakudhau kakudhau (sī॰) pakuddhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) kac‍cānau sañ‍cayau sañ‍jayau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bailaṭṭhaputtau bail‍laṭṭhiputtau (sī॰ pī॰), vaiḷaṭṭhaputtau (syā॰) nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nātaputtau (sī॰ pī॰), tai upasaṅkamitvā tai pañhai pucchati| tai sabhiyaina  paribbājakaina pañhai puṭṭhā na sampāyanti; asampāyantā kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraunti| api ca sabhiyaṃ yaiva paribbājakaṃ paṭipucchanti|

atha khau sabhiyassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yai khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, saiyyathidaṃ – pūraṇau kassapau…pai॰… nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau, tai mayā pañhai puṭṭhā na sampāyanti, asampāyantā kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraunti; api  ca mañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchanti| yan‍nūn‍nāhaṃ hīnāyāvattitvā kāmai paribhuñ‍jaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau sabhiyassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayampi khau samaṇau gautamau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa; yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā imai pañhai pucchaiyya’’nti|

atha khau sabhiyassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaipi khau tai yai khau tai (syā॰), yaṃ khau tai (ka॰) bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuḍḍhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, saiyyathidaṃ – pūraṇau kassapau…pai॰ … nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau, taipi mayā pañhai puṭṭhā na sampāyanti, asampāyantā kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraunti, api ca mañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchanti; kiṃ pana mai samaṇau gautamau imai pañhai puṭṭhau byākarissati! samaṇau hi gautamau daharau caiva jātiyā, navau ca pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti|

atha khau sabhiyassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘samaṇau khau samaṇau khau gautamau (syā॰ ka॰) daharauti na uñ‍ñātabbau na paribhautabbau| daharaupi caisa samaṇau gautamau mahiddhikau hauti mahānubhāvau, yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā imai pañhai pucchaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau sabhiyau paribbājakau yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ vaiḷuvanaṃ kalandakanivāpau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ  sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sabhiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

515.

 ‘‘kaṅkhī vaicikicchī āgamaṃ, (iti sabhiyau)

pañhai pucchituṃ abhikaṅkhamānau|

taisantakarau bhavāhi bhavāhi mai (pī॰ ka॰) pañhai mai puṭṭhau,

anupubbaṃ anudhammaṃ byākarauhi mai’’||

516.

 ‘‘dūratau  āgatausi sabhiya, (iti bhagavā)

pañhai pucchituṃ abhikaṅkhamānau|

taisantakarau bhavāmi taisamantakaraumi tai (ka॰) pañhai tai puṭṭhau,

anupubbaṃ anudhammaṃ byākaraumi tai||

517.

 ‘‘puccha  maṃ sabhiya pañhaṃ, yaṃ kiñ‍ci manasicchasi|

tassa tassaiva pañhassa, ahaṃ antaṃ karaumi tai’’ti||

atha khau sabhiyassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! yaṃ vatāhaṃ añ‍ñaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu aukāsakammamattampi aukāsamattampi (sī॰ pī॰) nālatthaṃ taṃ mai idaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina aukāsakammaṃ kata’’nti| attamanau pamuditau udaggau pītisaumanassajātau bhagavantaṃ  pañhaṃ apucchi –

518.

 ‘‘kiṃ pattinamāhu bhikkhunaṃ, (iti sabhiyau)

saurataṃ kaina kathañ‍ca dantamāhu|

buddhauti kathaṃ pavuc‍cati,

puṭṭhau mai bhagavā byākarauhi’’||

519.

 ‘‘paj‍jaina  kataina attanā, (sabhiyāti bhagavā)

parinibbānagatau vitiṇṇakaṅkhau|

vibhavañ‍ca bhavañ‍ca vippahāya,

vusitavā khīṇapunabbhavau sa bhikkhu||

520.

 ‘‘sabbattha  upaikkhakau satimā, na sau hiṃsati kañ‍ci sabbalaukai|

tiṇṇau samaṇau anāvilau, ussadā yassa na santi sauratau sau||

521.

 ‘‘yassindriyāni bhāvitāni, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

nibbijjha imaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ, kālaṃ kaṅkhati bhāvitau sa dantau||

522.

 ‘‘kappāni vicaiyya kaivalāni, saṃsāraṃ dubhayaṃ cutūpapātaṃ|

vigatarajamanaṅgaṇaṃ  visuddhaṃ, pattaṃ jātikhayaṃ tamāhu buddha’’nti||

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā attamanau pamuditau udaggau pītisaumanassajātau bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ uttari (ka॰) pañhaṃ apucchi –

523.

 ‘‘kiṃ  pattinamāhu brāhmaṇaṃ, (iti sabhiyau)

samaṇaṃ kaina kathañ‍ca nhātakauti|

nāgauti  kathaṃ pavuc‍cati,

puṭṭhau mai bhagavā byākarauhi’’||

524.

 ‘‘bāhitvā sabbapāpakāni, (sabhiyāti bhagavā)

vimalau sādhusamāhitau ṭhitattau|

saṃsāramatic‍ca kaivalī sau,

asitau tādi pavuc‍catai sa brahmā||

525.

 ‘‘samitāvi pahāya puñ‍ñapāpaṃ, virajau ñatvā imaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ|

jātimaraṇaṃ upātivattau, samaṇau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

526.

 ‘‘ninhāya  ninahāya (syā॰) sabbapāpakāni, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

daivamanussaisu  kappiyaisu, kappaṃ naiti tamāhu nhātakau’’ti||

527.

 ‘‘āguṃ na karauti kiñ‍ci laukai, sabbasaṃyaugai sabbayaugai (ka॰) visaj‍ja bandhanāni|

sabbattha na saj‍jatī vimuttau, nāgau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā’’ti||

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

528.

 ‘‘kaṃ khaittajinaṃ vadanti buddhā, (iti sabhiyau)

kusalaṃ kaina kathañ‍ca paṇḍitauti|

muni  nāma kathaṃ pavuc‍cati,

puṭṭhau mai bhagavā byākarauhi’’||

529.

 ‘‘khaittāni vicaiyya kaivalāni, (sabhiyāti bhagavā)

dibbaṃ mānusakañ‍ca brahmakhaittaṃ|

sabbakhaittamūlabandhanā pamuttau,

khaittajinau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

530.

 ‘‘kausāni  vicaiyya kaivalāni, dibbaṃ mānusakañ‍ca brahmakausaṃ|

sabbakausamūlabandhanā pamuttau, kusalau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

531.

 ‘‘dubhayāni  vicaiyya paṇḍarāni, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca suddhipañ‍ñau|

kaṇhaṃ suk‍kaṃ upātivattau, paṇḍitau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

532.

 ‘‘asatañ‍ca  satañ‍ca ñatvā dhammaṃ, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

daivamanussaihi pūjanīyau, saṅgaṃ jālamatic‍ca sau munī’’ti||

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

533.

 ‘‘kiṃ pattinamāhu  vaidaguṃ, (iti sabhiyau)

anuviditaṃ kaina kathañ‍ca vīriyavāti|

ājāniyau kinti nāma hauti,

puṭṭhau mai bhagavā byākarauhi’’||

534.

 ‘‘vaidāni vicaiyya kaivalāni, (sabhiyāti bhagavā)

samaṇānaṃ yānidhatthi yānipatthi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇānaṃ|

sabbavaidanāsu vītarāgau,

sabbaṃ vaidamatic‍ca vaidagū sau||

535.

 ‘‘anuvic‍ca papañ‍canāmarūpaṃ, ajjhattaṃ  bahiddhā ca raugamūlaṃ|

sabbaraugamūlabandhanā pamuttau, anuviditau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

536.

 ‘‘viratau idha sabbapāpakaihi, nirayadukkhaṃ atic‍ca vīriyavā sau|

sau vīriyavā padhānavā, dhīrau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

537.

 ‘‘yassassu lunāni bandhanāni, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca saṅgamūlaṃ|

sabbasaṅgamūlabandhanā pamuttau, ājāniyau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā’’ti||

atha  khau sabhiyau paribbājakau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

538.

 ‘‘kiṃ pattinamāhu sauttiyaṃ, (iti sabhiyau)

ariyaṃ  kaina kathañ‍ca caraṇavāti|

paribbājakau kinti nāma hauti,

puṭṭhau mai bhagavā byākarauhi’’||

539.

 ‘‘sutvā sabbadhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya laukai, (sabhiyāti bhagavā)

sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jaṃ yadatthi kiñ‍ci|

abhibhuṃ akathaṃkathiṃ vimuttaṃ,

anighaṃ  sabbadhimāhu sauttiyauti||

540.

 ‘‘chaitvā āsavāni ālayāni, vidvā sau na upaiti gabbhasaiyyaṃ|

sañ‍ñaṃ tividhaṃ panuj‍ja paṅkaṃ, kappaṃ naiti tamāhu ariyauti||

541.

 ‘‘yau idha caraṇaisu pattipattau, kusalau sabbadā ājānāti ājāni (syā॰) dhammaṃ|

sabbattha na saj‍jati vimuttacittau vimuttau (sī॰), paṭighā yassa na santi caraṇavā sau||

542.

 ‘‘dukkhavaipak‍kaṃ yadatthi kammaṃ, uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ vāpi tiriyañ‍cāpi (syā॰) majjhai|

paribbājayitvā pariñ‍ñacārī, māyaṃ mānamathaupi laubhakaudhaṃ|

pariyantamakāsi nāmarūpaṃ, taṃ paribbājakamāhu pattipatta’’nti||

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā attamanau pamuditau udaggau pītisaumanassajātau  uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

543.

 ‘‘yāni  ca tīṇi yāni ca saṭṭhi, samaṇappavādasitāni samaṇappavādanissitāni (syā॰ ka॰) bhūripañ‍ña|

sañ‍ñakkharasañ‍ñanissitāni, ausaraṇāni vinaiyya aughatamagā||

544.

 ‘‘antagūsi  pāragū pāragūsi (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) dukkhassa, arahāsi sammāsambuddhau khīṇāsavaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai|

jutimā mutimā pahūtapañ‍ñau, dukkhassantakaraṃ atāraisi maṃ||

545.

 ‘‘yaṃ mai kaṅkhitamañ‍ñāsi, vicikicchā maṃ tārayi namau tai|

muni maunapathaisu pattipatta, akhila ādic‍cabandhu sauratausi||

546.

 ‘‘yā  mai kaṅkhā purai āsi, taṃ mai byākāsi cakkhumā|

addhā munīsi sambuddhau, natthi nīvaraṇā tava||

547.

 ‘‘upāyāsā ca tai sabbai, viddhastā vinaḷīkatā|

sītibhūtau damappattau, dhitimā sac‍canik‍kamau||

548.

 ‘‘tassa tai nāganāgassa, mahāvīrassa bhāsatau|

sabbai daivānumaudanti, ubhau nāradapabbatā||

549.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi tai paṭipuggalau||

550.

 ‘‘tuvaṃ  buddhau tuvaṃ satthā, tuvaṃ mārābhibhū muni|

tuvaṃ anusayai chaitvā, tiṇṇau tāraisi maṃ pajaṃ||

551.

 ‘‘upadhī tai samatik‍kantā, āsavā tai padālitā|

sīhausi anupādānau, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

552.

 ‘‘puṇḍarīkaṃ  yathā vaggu, tauyai na upalimpati tauyaina na upalippati (sī॰), tauyai na upalippati (pī॰), tauyaina na upalimpati (ka॰)|

aivaṃ puñ‍ñai ca pāpai ca, ubhayai tvaṃ na limpasi|

pādai vīra pasāraihi, sabhiyau vandati satthunau’’ti||

atha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aisāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca; labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

‘‘yau khau, sabhiya, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, sau cattārau māsai parivasati; catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| api ca maittha puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhantā pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhantā  upasampadaṃ cattārau māsai parivasanti, catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya, ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi; catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti| alattha khau sabhiyau paribbājakau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ alattha upasampadaṃ…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā sabhiyau arahataṃ ahausīti|

sabhiyasuttaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. sailasuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi  bhikkhusataihi yaina āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅguttarāpānaṃ nigamau tadavasari| assausi  khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi āpaṇaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ  aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti bhagavā (syā॰ pī॰)| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisati ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ  parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau  aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni; tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu  abhippasan‍nau’’ti|

dutiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau; adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni; tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau’’ti|

tatiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhataiḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau, adhivāsaitu adhivāsaitvaiva (sī॰) mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina sakau assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mittāmac‍cai ñātisālauhitai āmantaisi – ‘‘suṇantu mai bhavantau mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā, samaṇau mai gautamau nimantitau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina, yaina mai kāyavaiyyāvaṭikaṃ karaiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa paṭissutvā appaikac‍cai uddhanāni khaṇanti, appaikac‍cai kaṭṭhāni phālainti, appaikac‍cai bhājanāni dhauvanti, appaikac‍cai udakamaṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāpainti, appaikac‍cai āsanāni pañ‍ñāpainti| kaiṇiyau pana jaṭilau sāmaṃyaiva maṇḍalamāḷaṃ  paṭiyādaiti|

taina khau pana samayaina sailau brāhmaṇau āpaṇai paṭivasati, tiṇṇaṃ  vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ  sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau, tīṇi ca māṇavakasatāni mantai vācaiti|

taina khau pana samayaina kaiṇiyau jaṭilau sailai brāhmaṇai abhippasan‍nau hauti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tīhi māṇavakasataihi parivutau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sailau brāhmaṇau kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamai kaiṇissamiyai jaṭilai (sī॰ pī॰) appaikac‍cai uddhanāni khaṇantai…pai॰… appaikac‍cai āsanāni pañ‍ñapaintai, kaiṇiyaṃ pana jaṭilaṃ sāmaṃyaiva maṇḍalamāḷaṃ paṭiyādaintaṃ| disvāna kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhautau kaiṇiyassa āvāhau vā bhavissati, vivāhau vā bhavissati, mahāyañ‍ñau vā pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyainā’’ti?

‘‘na mai, bhau saila, āvāhau vā bhavissati vivāhau vā, nāpi rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyaina; api ca khau mai mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau| atthi samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅguttarāpaisu cārikaṃ  caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi  bhikkhusataihi āpaṇaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ…pai॰… buddhau bhagavāti| sau mai nimantitau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| ‘‘buddhauti, bhau kaiṇiya, vadaisi’’? ‘‘buddhauti, bhau saila, vadāmi’’| ‘‘buddhauti, bhau kaiṇiya, vadaisi’’? ‘‘buddhauti, bhau saila, vadāmī’’ti|

atha khau sailassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ghausaupi khau aisau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ yadidaṃ buddhauti| āgatāni khau panamhākaṃ mantaisu dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati rājā hauti cak‍kavatti dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti, saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau vivattacchaddau (sī॰ pī॰)| kahaṃ pana, bhau kaiṇiya, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati  arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’’ti?

aivaṃ  vuttai, kaiṇiyau jaṭilau dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ paggahaitvā sailaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yainaisā, bhau saila, nīlavanarājī’’ti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tīhi māṇavakasataihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau tai māṇavakai āmantaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau āgacchantu, padai padaṃ nikkhipantā| durāsadā hi tai bhagavantau bhavantau (syā॰ ka॰) sīhāva aikacarā| yadā cāhaṃ, bhau, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ mantaiyyuṃ, mā mai bhauntau antarantarā kathaṃ aupātaitha; kathāpariyausānaṃ mai bhavantau āgamaintū’’ti|

atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau  kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi samman‍naisi (sī॰ syā॰)| addasā khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya cāti|

atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ sailau brāhmaṇau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya  cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi abhisaṅkhāraisi (syā॰ ka॰), yathā addasa sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni anumasi paṭimasi, ubhaupi nāsikasautāni anumasi paṭimasi, kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi|

atha khau sailassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saman‍nāgatau khau samaṇau gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi paripuṇṇaihi, nau apuripuṇṇaihi| nau ca khau naṃ jānāmi buddhau vā nau vā| sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yai tai bhavanti arahantau sammāsambuddhā, tai sakai vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attānaṃ pātukarauntī’ti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti| atha khau sailau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

553.

 ‘‘paripuṇṇakāyau suruci, sujātau cārudassanau|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇausi bhagavā, susuk‍kadāṭhausi vīriyavā||

554.

 ‘‘narassa  hi sujātassa, yai bhavanti viyañ‍janā|

sabbai tai tava kāyasmiṃ, mahāpurisalakkhaṇā||

555.

 ‘‘pasan‍nanaittau sumukhau, brahā uju patāpavā|

majjhai  samaṇasaṅghassa, ādic‍cauva viraucasi||

556.

 ‘‘kalyāṇadassanau  bhikkhu, kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau|

kiṃ tai samaṇabhāvaina, aivaṃ uttamavaṇṇinau||

557.

 ‘‘rājā arahasi bhavituṃ, cak‍kavattī rathaisabhau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambusaṇḍassa jambumaṇḍassa (ka॰) issarau||

558.

 ‘‘khattiyā  bhaugirājānau bhaujarājānau (sī॰ syā॰), anuyantā anuyuttā (sī॰) bhavantu tai|

rājābhirājā manujindau, raj‍jaṃ kāraihi gautama’’||

559.

 ‘‘rājāhamasmi sailāti, (bhagavā) dhammarājā anuttarau|

dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi, cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ’’||

560.

 ‘‘sambuddhau paṭijānāsi, (iti sailau brāhmaṇau) dhammarājā anuttarau|

‘dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi’, iti bhāsasi gautama||

561.

 ‘‘kau nu saināpati bhautau, sāvakau satthuranvayau|

kau tai tamanuvattaiti, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ’’||

562.

 ‘‘mayā pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā) dhammacak‍kaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāriputtau anuvattaiti, anujātau tathāgataṃ||

563.

 ‘‘abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñātaṃ, bhāvaitabbañ‍ca bhāvitaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ mai, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇa||

564.

 ‘‘vinayassu  mayi kaṅkhaṃ, adhimuc‍cassu brāhmaṇa|

dul‍labhaṃ dassanaṃ hauti, sambuddhānaṃ abhiṇhasau||

565.

 ‘‘yaisaṃ  vai yaisaṃ vau (pī॰), yassa vai (syā॰) dul‍labhau laukai, pātubhāvau abhiṇhasau|

sauhaṃ brāhmaṇa sambuddhau, sal‍lakattau anuttarau||

566.

 ‘‘brahmabhūtau atitulau, mārasainappamaddanau|

sabbāmittai vasīkatvā, maudāmi akutaubhayau’’||

567.

 ‘‘imaṃ bhavantau nisāmaitha, yathā bhāsati cakkhumā|

sal‍lakattau mahāvīrau, sīhauva nadatī vanai||

568.

 ‘‘brahmabhūtaṃ atitulaṃ, mārasainappamaddanaṃ|

kau disvā nappasīdaiyya, api kaṇhābhijātikau||

569.

 ‘‘yau maṃ icchati anvaitu, yau vā nicchati gacchatu|

idhāhaṃ pabbajissāmi, varapañ‍ñassa santikai’’||

570.

 ‘‘aivañ‍cai aitañ‍cai (sī॰ pī॰) ruc‍cati bhautau, sammāsambuddhasāsanai sammāsambuddhasāsanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

mayampi pabbajissāma, varapañ‍ñassa santikai’’||

571.

 ‘‘brāhmaṇā tisatā imai, yācanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

brahmacariyaṃ carissāma, bhagavā tava santikai’’||

572.

 ‘‘svākkhātaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā) sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ|

yattha amaughā pabbaj‍jā, appamattassa sikkhatau’’ti||

alattha khau sailau brāhmaṇau saparisau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau tassā  rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai assamai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau  kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina|

atha  khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

573.

 ‘‘aggihuttamukhā yañ‍ñā, sāvittī chandasau mukhaṃ|

rājā mukhaṃ manussānaṃ, nadīnaṃ sāgarau mukhaṃ||

574.

 ‘‘nakkhattānaṃ mukhaṃ candau, ādic‍cau tapataṃ mukhaṃ|

puñ‍ñaṃ ākaṅkhamānānaṃ, saṅghau vai yajataṃ mukha’’nti||

atha khau bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā sailau saparisau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassai  …pai॰…  añ‍ñatarau khau panāpasmā sailau saparisau arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau āyasmā sailau saparisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

575.

 ‘‘yaṃ taṃ saraṇamāgamha māgamma (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), itau aṭṭhami cakkhuma|

sattarattaina bhagavā, dantamha tava sāsanai||

576.

 ‘‘tuvaṃ buddhau tuvaṃ satthā, tuvaṃ mārābhibhū muni|

tuvaṃ anusayai chaitvā, tiṇṇau tāraisimaṃ pajaṃ||

577.

 ‘‘upadhī tai samatik‍kantā, āsavā tai padālitā|

sīhausi sīhauva (ma॰ ni॰ 2.401) anupādānau, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

578.

 ‘‘bhikkhavau  tisatā imai, tiṭṭhanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

pādai vīra pasāraihi, nāgā vandantu satthunau’’ti||

sailasuttaṃ sattamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. sal‍lasuttaṃ

579.

 animittamanañ‍ñātaṃ, mac‍cānaṃ idha jīvitaṃ|

kasirañ‍ca parittañ‍ca, tañ‍ca dukkhaina saṃyutaṃ||

580.

 na  hi sau upak‍kamau atthi, yaina jātā na miyyarai|

jarampi  patvā maraṇaṃ, aivaṃdhammā hi pāṇinau||

581.

 phalānamiva pak‍kānaṃ, pātau patanatau papatatau (sī॰ pī॰ aṭṭha॰) bhayaṃ|

aivaṃ jātāna mac‍cānaṃ, nic‍caṃ maraṇatau bhayaṃ||

582.

 yathāpi kumbhakārassa, katā mattikabhājanā|

sabbai bhaidanapariyantā bhaidapariyantā (syā॰), aivaṃ mac‍cāna jīvitaṃ||

583.

 daharā ca mahantā ca, yai bālā yai ca paṇḍitā|

sabbai mac‍cuvasaṃ yanti, sabbai mac‍cuparāyaṇā||

584.

 taisaṃ mac‍cuparaitānaṃ, gacchataṃ paralaukatau|

na pitā tāyatai puttaṃ, ñātī vā pana ñātakai||

585.

 paikkhataṃ yaiva ñātīnaṃ, passa lālapataṃ puthu|

aikamaikauva mac‍cānaṃ, gauvajjhau viya nīyati niyyati (bahūsu)||

586.

 aivamabbhāhatau  laukau, mac‍cunā ca jarāya ca|

tasmā dhīrā na saucanti, viditvā laukapariyāyaṃ||

587.

 yassa maggaṃ na jānāsi, āgatassa gatassa vā|

ubhau antai asampassaṃ, niratthaṃ paridaivasi||

588.

 paridaivayamānau cai, kiñ‍cidatthaṃ udabbahai|

sammūḷhau hiṃsamattānaṃ, kayirā cai naṃ vicakkhaṇau||

589.

 na hi ruṇṇaina saukaina, santiṃ pappauti caitasau|

bhiyyassuppaj‍jatai dukkhaṃ, sarīraṃ cupahañ‍ñati||

590.

 kisau  vivaṇṇau bhavati, hiṃsamattānamattanā|

na  taina paitā pālainti, niratthā paridaivanā||

591.

 saukamappajahaṃ  jantu, bhiyyau dukkhaṃ nigacchati|

anutthunantau kālaṅkataṃ kālakataṃ (sī॰ syā॰), saukassa vasamanvagū||

592.

 añ‍ñaipi passa gaminai, yathākammūpagai narai|

mac‍cunau vasamāgamma, phandantaividha pāṇinau||

593.

 yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā|

aitādisau vinābhāvau, passa laukassa pariyāyaṃ||

594.

 api vassasataṃ jīvai, bhiyyau vā pana māṇavau|

ñātisaṅghā vinā hauti, jahāti idha jīvitaṃ||

595.

 tasmā arahatau sutvā, vinaiyya paridaivitaṃ|

paitaṃ kālaṅkataṃ disvā, naisau labbhā mayā iti||

596.

 yathā  saraṇamādittaṃ, vārinā parinibbayai parinibbutau (sī॰ ka॰)|

aivampi dhīrau sapañ‍ñau, paṇḍitau kusalau narau|

khippamuppatitaṃ saukaṃ, vātau tūlaṃva dhaṃsayai||

597.

 paridaivaṃ pajappañ‍ca, daumanassañ‍ca attanau|

attanau sukhamaisānau, abbahai sal‍lamattanau||

598.

 abbuḷhasal‍lau asitau, santiṃ pappuyya caitasau|

sabbasaukaṃ atik‍kantau, asaukau hauti nibbutauti||

sal‍lasuttaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. vāsaiṭṭhasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā brāhmaṇamahāsālā icchānaṅgalai paṭivasanti, saiyyathidaṃ – caṅkī brāhmaṇau, tārukkhau brāhmaṇau, paukkharasāti brāhmaṇau, jāṇussauṇi jāṇusauṇi (ka॰) brāhmaṇau, taudaiyyau brāhmaṇau, añ‍ñai ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā brāhmaṇamahāsālā| atha khau vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājānaṃ māṇavānaṃ  jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamantānaṃ anuvicarantānaṃ anucaṅkamamānānaṃ anuvicaramānānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘kathaṃ, bhau, brāhmaṇau hautī’’ti?

bhāradvājau māṇavau aivamāha – ‘‘yatau khau, bhau, ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, aittāvatā khau bhau brāhmaṇau hautī’’ti|

vāsaiṭṭhau māṇavau aivamāha – ‘‘yatau khau, bhau, sīlavā ca hauti vatasampan‍nau vattasampan‍nau (sī॰ syā॰ ma॰ ni॰ 2.454) ca, aittāvatā khau, bhau, brāhmaṇau hautī’’ti| naiva khau asakkhi bhāradvājau māṇavau vāsaiṭṭhaṃ  māṇavaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ, na pana asakkhi vāsaiṭṭhau māṇavau bhāradvājaṃ māṇavaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ|

atha  khau vāsaiṭṭhau māṇavau bhāradvājaṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, bhau ayaṃ bhau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), ayaṃ khau (pī॰) bhāradvāja, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā  pabbajitau icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai; taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti| āyāma, bhau bhāradvāja, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchissāma| yathā nau samaṇau gautamau byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau bhāradvājau māṇavau vāsaiṭṭhassa māṇavassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā māṇavā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vāsaiṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

599.

 ‘‘anuñ‍ñātapaṭiñ‍ñātā, taivij‍jā mayamasmubhau|

ahaṃ paukkharasātissa, tārukkhassāyaṃ māṇavau||

600.

 ‘‘taivij‍jānaṃ yadakkhātaṃ, tatra kaivalinausmasai|

padakasma vaiyyākaraṇā, jappai ācariyasādisā||

601.

 ‘‘taisaṃ  nau jātivādasmiṃ, vivādau atthi gautama|

jātiyā brāhmaṇau hauti, bhāradvājau iti bhāsati|

ahañ‍ca kammunā kammanā (sī॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi brūmi, aivaṃ jānāhi cakkhuma||

602.

 ‘‘tai na sak‍kauma sañ‍ñāpaituṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mayaṃ ubhau|

bhavantaṃ bhagavantaṃ (ka॰) puṭṭhumāgamhā, sambuddhaṃ iti vissutaṃ||

603.

 ‘‘candaṃ  yathā khayātītaṃ, paic‍ca pañ‍jalikā janā|

vandamānā namassanti, aivaṃ laukasmi gautamaṃ||

604.

 ‘‘cakkhuṃ laukai samuppan‍naṃ, mayaṃ pucchāma gautamaṃ|

jātiyā brāhmaṇau hauti, udāhu bhavati kammunā|

ajānataṃ nau pabrūhi, yathā jānaisu brāhmaṇaṃ’’||

605.

 ‘‘taisaṃ  vau ahaṃ byakkhissaṃ, (vāsaiṭṭhāti bhagavā) anupubbaṃ yathātathaṃ|

jātivibhaṅgaṃ pāṇānaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

606.

 ‘‘tiṇarukkhaipi jānātha, na cāpi paṭijānarai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

607.

 ‘‘tatau  kīṭai paṭaṅgai ca, yāva kunthakipil‍likai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

608.

 ‘‘catuppadaipi jānātha, khuddakai ca mahal‍lakai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

609.

 ‘‘pādūdaraipi jānātha, uragai dīghapiṭṭhikai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

610.

 ‘‘tatau macchaipi jānātha, audakai vārigaucarai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

611.

 ‘‘tatau pakkhīpi jānātha, pattayānai vihaṅgamai|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ taisaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñā hi jātiyau||

612.

 ‘‘yathā  aitāsu jātīsu, liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ puthu|

aivaṃ natthi manussaisu, liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ puthu||

613.

 ‘‘na kaisaihi na sīsaina, na kaṇṇaihi na akkhibhi|

na mukhaina na nāsāya, na auṭṭhaihi bhamūhi vā||

614.

 ‘‘na gīvāya na aṃsaihi, na udaraina na piṭṭhiyā|

na sauṇiyā na urasā, na sambādhai na maithunai na sambādhā na maithunā (syā॰ ka॰)||

615.

 ‘‘na  hatthaihi na pādaihi, nāṅgulīhi nakhaihi vā|

na jaṅghāhi na ūrūhi, na vaṇṇaina saraina vā|

liṅgaṃ jātimayaṃ naiva, yathā añ‍ñāsu jātisu||

616.

 ‘‘pac‍cattañ‍ca  sarīraisu pac‍cattaṃ sasarīraisu (sī॰ pī॰), manussaisvaitaṃ na vij‍jati|

vaukārañ‍ca manussaisu, samañ‍ñāya pavuc‍cati||

617.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, gaurakkhaṃ upajīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, kassakau sau na brāhmaṇau||

618.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, puthusippaina jīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, sippikau sau na brāhmaṇau||

619.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, vauhāraṃ upajīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, vāṇijau sau na brāhmaṇau||

620.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, parapaissaina jīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, paissikau paissakau (ka॰) sau na brāhmaṇau||

621.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, adin‍naṃ upajīvati|

aivaṃ  vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, caurau aisau na brāhmaṇau||

622.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, issatthaṃ upajīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, yaudhājīvau na brāhmaṇau||

623.

 ‘‘yau hi kauci manussaisu, paurauhic‍caina jīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, yājakau aisau na brāhmaṇau||

624.

 ‘‘yau  hi kauci manussaisu, gāmaṃ raṭṭhañ‍ca bhuñ‍jati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, rājā aisau na brāhmaṇau||

625.

 ‘‘na  cāhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brūmi, yaunijaṃ mattisambhavaṃ|

bhauvādi nāma sau hauti, sacai sa vai (sī॰ syā॰) hauti sakiñ‍canau|

akiñ‍canaṃ anādānaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

626.

 ‘‘sabbasaṃyaujanaṃ chaitvā, sau vai na paritassati|

saṅgātigaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

627.

 ‘‘chaitvā  naddhiṃ varattañ‍ca, sandānaṃ sahanuk‍kamaṃ|

ukkhittapalighaṃ buddhaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

628.

 ‘‘ak‍kausaṃ vadhabandhañ‍ca, aduṭṭhau yau titikkhati|

khantībalaṃ balānīkaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

629.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaṃ vatavantaṃ, sīlavantaṃ anussadaṃ|

dantaṃ antimasārīraṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

630.

 ‘‘vāri paukkharapattaiva, āraggairiva sāsapau|

yau na limpati kāmaisu, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

631.

 ‘‘yau  dukkhassa pajānāti, idhaiva khayamattanau|

pan‍nabhāraṃ visaṃyuttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

632.

 ‘‘gambhīrapañ‍ñaṃ maidhāviṃ, maggāmaggassa kauvidaṃ|

uttamatthamanuppattaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

633.

 ‘‘asaṃsaṭṭhaṃ gahaṭṭhaihi, anāgāraihi cūbhayaṃ|

anaukasārimappicchaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

634.

 ‘‘nidhāya daṇḍaṃ bhūtaisu, tasaisu thāvaraisu ca|

yau na hanti na ghātaiti, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

635.

 ‘‘aviruddhaṃ  viruddhaisu, attadaṇḍaisu nibbutaṃ|

sādānaisu anādānaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

636.

 ‘‘yassa  rāgau ca dausau ca, mānau makkhau ca pātitau|

sāsapauriva āraggā, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

637.

 ‘‘akak‍kasaṃ  viñ‍ñāpaniṃ, giraṃ sac‍camudīrayai|

yāya nābhisajai kañ‍ci, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

638.

 ‘‘yaudha dīghaṃ va rassaṃ vā, aṇuṃ thūlaṃ subhāsubhaṃ|

laukai adin‍naṃ nādiyati, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

639.

 ‘‘āsā yassa na vij‍janti, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca|

nirāsāsaṃ nirāsayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), nirāsakaṃ (?) visaṃyuttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

640.

 ‘‘yassālayā na vij‍janti, añ‍ñāya akathaṃkathī|

amataugadhamanuppattaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

641.

 ‘‘yaudha  puñ‍ñañ‍ca pāpañ‍ca, ubhau saṅgamupac‍cagā|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ suddhaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

642.

 ‘‘candaṃva vimalaṃ suddhaṃ, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ|

nandībhavaparikkhīṇaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

643.

 ‘‘yaumaṃ palipathaṃ duggaṃ, saṃsāraṃ mauhamac‍cagā|

tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau jhāyī, anaijau akathaṃkathī|

anupādāya nibbutau, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

644.

 ‘‘yaudha kāmai pahantvāna, anāgārau paribbajai|

kāmabhavaparikkhīṇaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

645.

 ‘‘yaudha  taṇhaṃ pahantvāna, anāgārau paribbajai|

taṇhābhavaparikkhīṇaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

646.

 ‘‘hitvā mānusakaṃ yaugaṃ, dibbaṃ yaugaṃ upac‍cagā|

sabbayaugavisaṃyuttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

647.

 ‘‘hitvā ratiñ‍ca aratiṃ, sītibhūtaṃ nirūpadhiṃ|

sabbalaukābhibhuṃ vīraṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

648.

 ‘‘cutiṃ  yau vaidi yau’vaiti (?) itivuttakai 99 aṭṭhakathāsaṃvaṇanā passitabbā ttānaṃ, upapattiñ‍ca sabbasau|

asattaṃ sugataṃ buddhaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

649.

 ‘‘yassa gatiṃ na jānanti, daivā gandhabbamānusā|

khīṇāsavaṃ arahantaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

650.

 ‘‘yassa purai ca pacchā ca, majjhai ca natthi kiñ‍canaṃ|

akiñ‍canaṃ  anādānaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

651.

 ‘‘usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ, mahaisiṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

anaijaṃ nhātakaṃ buddhaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

652.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ yau vaidi yau’vaiti (?) itivuttakai 99 aṭṭhakathāsaṃvaṇanā passitabbā, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ||

653.

 ‘‘samañ‍ñā haisā laukasmiṃ, nāmagauttaṃ pakappitaṃ|

sammuc‍cā samudāgataṃ, tattha tattha pakappitaṃ||

654.

 ‘‘dīgharattamanusayitaṃ, diṭṭhigatamajānataṃ|

ajānantā nau ajānantā tai (aṭṭha॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.460 pabruvanti, jātiyā hauti brāhmaṇau||

655.

 ‘‘na  jac‍cā brāhmaṇau hauti, na jac‍cā hauti abrāhmaṇau|

kammunā brāhmaṇau hauti, kammunā hauti abrāhmaṇau||

656.

 ‘‘kassakau kammunā hauti, sippikau hauti kammunā|

vāṇijau kammunā hauti, paissikau hauti kammunā||

657.

 ‘‘cauraupi kammunā hauti, yaudhājīvaupi kammunā|

yājakau kammunā hauti, rājāpi hauti kammunā||

658.

 ‘‘aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ, kammaṃ passanti paṇḍitā|

paṭic‍casamuppādadassā, kammavipākakauvidā||

659.

 ‘‘kammunā vattati laukau, kammunā vattati pajā|

kammanibandhanā sattā, rathassāṇīva yāyatau||

660.

 ‘‘tapaina brahmacariyaina, saṃyamaina damaina ca|

aitaina  brāhmaṇau hauti, aitaṃ brāhmaṇamuttamaṃ||

661.

 ‘‘tīhi vij‍jāhi sampan‍nau, santau khīṇapunabbhavau|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāhi, brahmā sak‍kau vijānata’’nti||

aivaṃ vuttai, vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā māṇavā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai pāṇupaitaṃ (ka॰) saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

vāsaiṭṭhasuttaṃ navamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. kaukālikasuttaṃ

aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pāpicchā, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, mā haivaṃ, kaukālika! pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰…  tatiyampi khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi mai, bhantai, bhagavā saddhāyikau pac‍cayikau, atha khau pāpicchāva sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, mā haivaṃ, kaukālika! pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| acirappak‍kantassa ca kaukālikassa bhikkhunau sāsapamattīhi piḷakāhi sabbau kāyau phuṭau phuṭṭhau (syā॰) ahausi; sāsapamattiyau hutvā muggamattiyau ahaisuṃ; muggamattiyau hutvā kaḷāyamattiyau ahaisuṃ; kaḷāyamattiyau hutvā kaulaṭṭhimattiyau ahaisuṃ; kaulaṭṭhimattiyau  hutvā kaulamattiyau ahaisuṃ; kaulamattiyau hutvā āmalakamattiyau ahaisuṃ; āmalakamattiyau hutvā baiḷuvasalāṭukamattiyau ahaisuṃ; baiḷuvasalāṭukamattiyau hutvā bil‍lamattiyau ahaisuṃ; bil‍lamattiyau hutvā pabhij‍jiṃsu; pubbañ‍ca lauhitañ‍ca pagghariṃsu| atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu tainaivābādhaina kālamakāsi| kālaṅkatau ca kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍ji sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā|

atha khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ, ṭhitau khau brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaukālikau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; kālaṅkatau ca, bhantai, kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati; idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā…pai॰… idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvā maṃ padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvadīghaṃ nu khau, bhantai, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇa’’nti? ‘‘dīghaṃ khau, bhikkhu, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇaṃ; taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ aittakāni vassāni iti vā aittakāni vassasatāni iti vā aittakāni vassasahassāni  iti vā aittakāni vassasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti| ‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamā upamaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau; tatau purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina aikamaikaṃ tilaṃ uddharaiyya| khippataraṃ khau sau bhikkhu vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau  iminā upak‍kamaina parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, natvaiva aikau abbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati abbudā nirayā aivamaikau nirabbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati nirabbudā nirayā aivamaikau ababau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ababā nirayā aivamaikau ahahau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ahahā nirayā aivamaikau aṭaṭau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā aivamaikau kumudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati kumudā nirayā aivamaikau saugandhikau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati saugandhikā nirayā aivamaikau uppalakau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati uppalakā nirayā aivamaikau puṇḍarīkau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati puṇḍarīkā nirayā aivamaikau padumau nirayau| padumaṃ khau pana bhikkhu nirayaṃ kaukālikau bhikkhu upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

662.

 ‘‘purisassa  hi jātassa, kuṭhārī kudhārī (ka॰) jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

663.

 ‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati, taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ, kalinā  taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

664.

 ‘‘appamattau  ayaṃ kali, yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā, ayamaiva mahattarau mahantakarau (sī॰) kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

665.

 ‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ, chattiṃsati pañ‍ca ca abbudāni abbudānaṃ (ka॰)|

yamariyagarahī nirayaṃ upaiti, vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpakaṃ||

666.

 ‘‘abhūtavādī nirayaṃ upaiti, yau vāpi katvā na karaumicāha|

ubhaupi tai paic‍ca samā bhavanti, nihīnakammā manujā parattha||

667.

 ‘‘yau appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati, suddhassa pausassa anaṅgaṇassa|

tamaiva bālaṃ pac‍caiti pāpaṃ, sukhumau rajau paṭivātaṃva khittau||

668.

 ‘‘yau laubhaguṇai anuyuttau, sau vacasā paribhāsati añ‍ñai|

asaddhau  kadariyau avadañ‍ñū, macchari paisuṇiyaṃ paisuṇiyasmiṃ (bahūsu) anuyuttau||

669.

 ‘‘mukhadugga vibhūta anariya, bhūnahu bhunahata (syā॰ ka॰) pāpaka duk‍kaṭakāri|

purisanta kalī avajāta, mā bahubhāṇidha nairayikausi||

670.

 ‘‘rajamākirasī  ahitāya, santai garahasi kibbisakārī|

bahūni duc‍caritāni caritvā, gacchasi khau papataṃ cirarattaṃ||

671.

 ‘‘na hi nassati kassaci kammaṃ, aiti hataṃ labhataiva suvāmi|

dukkhaṃ mandau paralaukai, attani passati kibbisakārī||

672.

 ‘‘ayausaṅkusamāhataṭṭhānaṃ, tiṇhadhāramayasūlamupaiti|

atha  tattaayauguḷasan‍nibhaṃ, bhaujanamatthi tathā patirūpaṃ||

673.

 ‘‘na hi vaggu vadanti vadantā, nābhijavanti na tāṇamupainti|

aṅgārai  santhatai sayanti sainti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ginisampaj‍jalitaṃ pavisanti||

674.

 ‘‘jālaina ca aunahiyāna, tattha hananti ayaumayakuṭaibhi ayaumayakūṭaihi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

andhaṃva timisamāyanti, taṃ vitatañhi yathā mahikāyau||

675.

 ‘‘atha lauhamayaṃ pana kumbhiṃ, ginisampaj‍jalitaṃ pavisanti|

pac‍canti hi tāsu cirarattaṃ, agginisamāsu ginissamāsu (ka॰) samuppilavātai||

676.

 ‘‘atha pubbalauhitamissai, tattha kiṃ pac‍cati kibbisakārī|

yaṃ yaṃ  disakaṃ disataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) adhisaiti, tattha kilissati samphusamānau||

677.

 ‘‘puḷavāvasathai  salilasmiṃ, tattha kiṃ pac‍cati kibbisakārī|

gantuṃ na hi tīramapatthi, sabbasamā hi samantakapal‍lā||

678.

 ‘‘asipattavanaṃ pana tiṇhaṃ, taṃ pavisanti samucchidagattā|

jivhaṃ  balisaina gahaitvā, ārajayārajayā vihananti||

679.

 ‘‘atha vaitaraṇiṃ pana duggaṃ, tiṇhadhārakhuradhāramupainti|

tattha mandā papatanti, pāpakarā pāpāni karitvā||

680.

 ‘‘khādanti  hi tattha rudantai, sāmā sabalā kākaulagaṇā ca|

sauṇā siṅgālā sigālā (sī॰ pī॰) paṭigiddhā paṭigijjhā (syā॰ pī॰), kulalā vāyasā ca kulalā ca vāyasā (?) vitudanti||

681.

 ‘‘kicchā vatayaṃ idha vutti, yaṃ janau phusati passati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kibbisakārī|

tasmā idha jīvitasaisai, kic‍cakarau siyā narau na cappamaj‍jai||

682.

 ‘‘tai  gaṇitā vidūhi tilavāhā, yai padumai nirayai upanītā|

nahutāni hi kauṭiyau pañ‍ca bhavanti, dvādasa kauṭisatāni punañ‍ñā panayyai (ka॰)||

683.

 ‘‘yāva  dukhā dukkhā (sī॰ syā॰), dukkha (pī॰ ka॰) nirayā idha vuttā, tatthapi tāva ciraṃ vasitabbaṃ|

tasmā  sucipaisalasādhuguṇaisu, vācaṃ manaṃ satataṃ pakataṃ (syā॰) parirakkhai’’ti||

kaukālikasuttaṃ dasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. nālakasuttaṃ

684.

 ānandajātai tidasagaṇai patītai, sak‍kañ‍ca indaṃ sucivasanai ca daivai|

dussaṃ gahaitvā atiriva thaumayantai, asitau isi addasa divāvihārai||

685.

 disvāna  daivai muditamanai udaggai, cittiṃ karitvāna idamavauca karitvā idamavaucāsi (sī॰) tattha|

‘‘kiṃ daivasaṅghau atiriva kalyarūpau, dussaṃ gahaitvā ramayatha bhamayatha (sī॰) kiṃ paṭic‍ca||

686.

 ‘‘yadāpi āsī asuraihi saṅgamau, jayau surānaṃ asurā parājitā||

tadāpi naitādisau laumahaṃsanau, kimabbhutaṃ daṭṭhu marū pamauditā||

687.

 ‘‘saiḷainti gāyanti ca vādayanti ca, bhujāni phauṭainti pauṭhainti (sī॰ pī॰), pauthainti (ka॰) ca nac‍cayanti ca|

pucchāmi  vauhaṃ mairumuddhavāsinai, dhunātha mai saṃsayaṃ khippa mārisā’’||

688.

 ‘‘sau  baudhisattau ratanavarau atulyau, manussalaukai hitasukhatthāya hitasukhatāya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) jātau|

sakyāna gāmai janapadai lumbinaiyyai, tainamha tuṭṭhā atiriva kalyarūpā||

689.

 ‘‘sau sabbasattuttamau aggapuggalau, narāsabhau sabbapajānamuttamau|

vattaissati  cak‍kamisivhayai vanai, nadaṃva sīhau balavā migābhibhū’’||

690.

 taṃ  saddaṃ sutvā turitamavasarī sau, suddhaudanassa tada bhavanaṃ upāvisi upāgami (sī॰ pī॰)|

nisaj‍ja tattha idamavaucāsi sakyai, ‘‘kuhiṃ kumārau ahamapi daṭṭhukāmau’’||

691.

 tatau kumāraṃ jalitamiva suvaṇṇaṃ, uk‍kāmukhaiva sukusalasampahaṭṭhaṃ sukusalaina sampahaṭṭhaṃ (ka॰)|

daddal‍lamānaṃ daddaḷhamānaṃ (ka॰) siriyā anaumavaṇṇaṃ, dassaisu puttaṃ asitavhayassa sakyā||

692.

 disvā kumāraṃ sikhimiva paj‍jalantaṃ, tārāsabhaṃva  nabhasigamaṃ visuddhaṃ|

sūriyaṃ tapantaṃ saradarivabbhamuttaṃ, ānandajātau vipulamalattha pītiṃ||

693.

 anaikasākhañ‍ca sahassamaṇḍalaṃ, chattaṃ marū dhārayumantalikkhai|

suvaṇṇadaṇḍā vītipatanti cāmarā, na dissarai cāmarachattagāhakā||

694.

 disvā  jaṭī kaṇhasirivhayau isi, suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ viya paṇḍukambalai|

saitañ‍ca chattaṃ dhariyanta dhāriyanta (syā॰), dhārayantaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) muddhani, udaggacittau sumanau paṭiggahai||

695.

 paṭiggahaitvā pana sakyapuṅgavaṃ, jigīsatau jigiṃsakau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) lakkhaṇamantapāragū|

pasan‍nacittau giramabbhudīrayi, ‘‘anuttarāyaṃ dvipadānamuttamau’’ dipadānamuttamau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

696.

 athattanau gamanamanussarantau, akalyarūpau gaḷayati assukāni|

disvāna sakyā isimavaucuṃ rudantaṃ,

‘‘nau cai kumārai bhavissati antarāyau’’||

697.

 disvāna sakyai isimavauca akalyai, ‘‘nāhaṃ  kumārai ahitamanussarāmi|

na cāpimassa bhavissati antarāyau, na aurakāyaṃ adhimānasā adhimanasā (sī॰ syā॰) bhavātha||

698.

 ‘‘sambaudhiyaggaṃ phusissatāyaṃ kumārau, sau dhammacak‍kaṃ paramavisuddhadassī|

vattaissatāyaṃ bahujanahitānukampī, vitthārikassa bhavissati brahmacariyaṃ||

699.

 ‘‘mamañ‍ca  āyu na ciramidhāvasaisau, athantarā mai bhavissati kālakiriyā|

sauhaṃ na saussaṃ sussaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) asamadhurassa dhammaṃ, tainamhi aṭṭau byasanaṃgatau aghāvī’’||

700.

 sau  sākiyānaṃ vipulaṃ janaitvā pītiṃ, antaipuramhā niggamā niragamā (sī॰ syā॰), nigamā (ka॰ sī॰), niragama (pī॰) brahmacārī|

sau bhāginaiyyaṃ sayaṃ anukampamānau, samādapaisi asamadhurassa dhammai||

701.

 ‘‘buddhauti ghausaṃ yada yadi (syā॰ ka॰) paratau suṇāsi, sambaudhipattau vivarati dhammamaggaṃ|

gantvāna tattha samayaṃ paripucchamānau sayaṃ paripucchiyānau (sī॰ syā॰), carassu tasmiṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ’’||

702.

 tainānusiṭṭhau hitamanaina tādinā, anāgatai  paramavisuddhadassinā|

sau nālakau upacitapuñ‍ñasañ‍cayau, jinaṃ patikkhaṃ pati + ikkhaṃ = patikkhaṃ parivasi rakkhitindriyau||

703.

 sutvāna ghausaṃ jinavaracak‍kavattanai, gantvāna disvā isinisabhaṃ pasan‍nau|

maunaiyyasaiṭṭhaṃ  munipavaraṃ apucchi, samāgatai asitāvhayassa sāsanaiti||

vatthugāthā niṭṭhitā|

704.

 ‘‘añ‍ñātamaitaṃ vacanaṃ, asitassa yathātathaṃ|

taṃ taṃ gautama pucchāmi, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

705.

 ‘‘anagāriyupaitassa, bhikkhācariyaṃ jigīsatau|

muni pabrūhi mai puṭṭhau, maunaiyyaṃ uttamaṃ padaṃ’’||

706.

 ‘‘maunaiyyaṃ tai upañ‍ñissaṃ, (iti bhagavā) duk‍karaṃ durabhisambhavaṃ|

handa tai naṃ pavakkhāmi, santhambhassu daḷhau bhava||

707.

 ‘‘samānabhāgaṃ  kubbaitha, gāmai ak‍kuṭṭhavanditaṃ|

manaupadausaṃ rakkhaiyya, santau anuṇṇatau carai||

708.

 ‘‘uc‍cāvacā  niccharanti, dāyai aggisikhūpamā|

nāriyau muniṃ palaubhainti, tāsu taṃ mā palaubhayuṃ||

709.

 ‘‘viratau maithunā dhammā, hitvā kāmai parauparai parauvarai (sī॰ pī॰), varāvarai (syā॰)|

aviruddhau  asārattau, pāṇaisu tasathāvarai||

710.

 ‘‘yathā ahaṃ tathā aitai, yathā aitai tathā ahaṃ|

attānaṃ upamaṃ katvā, na hanaiyya na ghātayai||

711.

 ‘‘hitvā icchañ‍ca laubhañ‍ca, yattha sattau puthuj‍janau|

cakkhumā paṭipaj‍jaiyya, taraiyya narakaṃ imaṃ||

712.

 ‘‘ūnūdarau mitāhārau, appicchassa alaulupau|

sadā sa vai (pī॰) icchāya nicchātau, anicchau hauti nibbutau||

713.

 ‘‘sa piṇḍacāraṃ caritvā, vanantamabhihārayai|

upaṭṭhitau rukkhamūlasmiṃ, āsanūpagatau muni||

714.

 ‘‘sa jhānapasutau dhīrau, vanantai ramitau siyā|

jhāyaitha rukkhamūlasmiṃ, attānamabhitausayaṃ||

715.

 ‘‘tatau ratyā vivasānai vivasanai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), gāmantamabhihārayai|

avhānaṃ nābhinandaiyya, abhihārañ‍ca gāmatau||

716.

 ‘‘na munī gāmamāgamma, kulaisu sahasā carai|

ghāsaisanaṃ chin‍nakathau, na vācaṃ payutaṃ bhaṇai||

717.

 ‘‘alatthaṃ yadidaṃ sādhu, nālatthaṃ kusalaṃ iti|

ubhayainaiva sau tādī, rukkhaṃvupanivattati rukkhaṃvu’pativattati (ka॰), rukkhaṃva upātivattati (syā॰)||

718.

 ‘‘sa  pattapāṇi vicarantau, amūgau mūgasammatau|

appaṃ dānaṃ na hīḷaiyya, dātāraṃ nāvajāniyā||

719.

 ‘‘uc‍cāvacā  hi paṭipadā, samaṇaina pakāsitā|

na pāraṃ  diguṇaṃ yanti, nayidaṃ aikaguṇaṃ mutaṃ||

720.

 ‘‘yassa ca visatā natthi, chin‍nasautassa bhikkhunau|

kic‍cākic‍cappahīnassa, pariḷāhau na vij‍jati||

721.

 ‘‘maunaiyyaṃ tai upañ‍ñissaṃ, khuradhārūpamau bhavai|

jivhāya tālumāhac‍ca, udarai sañ‍ñatau siyā||

722.

 ‘‘alīnacittau ca siyā, na cāpi bahu cintayai|

nirāmagandhau asitau, brahmacariyaparāyaṇau||

723.

 ‘‘aikāsanassa sikkhaitha, samaṇūpāsanassa ca|

aikattaṃ maunamakkhātaṃ, aikau cai abhiramissasi|

atha bhāhisi bhāsihi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dasadisā||

724.

 ‘‘sutvā dhīrānaṃ nigghausaṃ, jhāyīnaṃ kāmacāginaṃ|

tatau hiriñ‍ca saddhañ‍ca, bhiyyau kubbaitha māmakau||

725.

 ‘‘taṃ  nadīhi vijānātha, saubbhaisu padaraisu ca|

saṇantā yanti kusaubbhā kussubbhā (sī॰), tuṇhīyanti mahaudadhī||

726.

 ‘‘yadūnakaṃ taṃ saṇati, yaṃ pūraṃ santamaiva taṃ|

aḍḍhakumbhūpamau bālau, rahadau pūrauva paṇḍitau||

727.

 ‘‘yaṃ samaṇau bahuṃ bhāsati, upaitaṃ atthasañhitaṃ|

jānaṃ sau dhammaṃ daisaiti, jānaṃ sau bahu bhāsati||

728.

 ‘‘yau  ca jānaṃ saṃyatattau, jānaṃ na bahu bhāsati|

sa  munī maunamarahati, sa munī maunamajjhagā’’ti||

nālakasuttaṃ aikādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. dvayatānupassanāsuttaṃ

aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai  puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, kusalā dhammā ariyā niyyānikā sambaudhagāminau, taisaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ariyānaṃ niyyānikānaṃ sambaudhagāmīnaṃ kā upanisā savanāyā’ti iti cai, bhikkhavai, pucchitārau assu, tai aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘yāvadaiva dvayatānaṃ dhammānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāyā’ti| kiñ‍ca dvayataṃ vadaitha?

(1) ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti ayamaikānupassanā| ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā dvayatānupassinau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā  upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

729.

 ‘‘yai dukkhaṃ nappajānanti, athau dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

yattha ca sabbasau dukkhaṃ, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

tañ‍ca maggaṃ na jānanti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

730.

 ‘‘caitauvimuttihīnā tai, athau pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā|

abhabbā tai antakiriyāya, tai vai jātijarūpagā||

731.

 ‘‘yai  ca dukkhaṃ pajānanti, athau dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

yattha ca sabbasau dukkhaṃ, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

tañ‍ca  maggaṃ pajānanti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

732.

 ‘‘caitauvimuttisampan‍nā, athau pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā|

bhabbā tai antakiriyāya, na tai jātijarūpagā’’ti||

(2) ‘‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi pariyāyaina sammā dvayatānupassanā’ti, iti cai, bhikkhavai, pucchitārau assu; ‘siyā’tissu vacanīyā| kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ upadhipac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| upadhīnaṃ  tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

733.

 ‘‘upadhinidānā pabhavanti dukkhā, yai  kaici laukasmimanaikarūpā|

yau vai avidvā upadhiṃ karauti, punappunaṃ dukkhamupaiti mandau|

tasmā pajānaṃ upadhiṃ na kayirā, dukkhassa jātippabhavānupassī’’ti||

(3) ‘‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi pariyāyaina sammā dvayatānupassanā’ti, iti cai, bhikkhavai, pucchitārau assu; ‘siyā’tissu vacanīyā| kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

734.

 ‘‘jātimaraṇasaṃsāraṃ, yai vajanti punappunaṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, avij‍jāyaiva sā gati||

735.

 ‘‘avij‍jā hāyaṃ mahāmauhau, yainidaṃ saṃsitaṃ ciraṃ|

vij‍jāgatā ca yai sattā, na tai gacchanti nāgacchanti (sī॰ pī॰) punabbhava’’nti||

(4) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| saṅkhārānaṃ tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

736.

 ‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti, sabbaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā|

saṅkhārānaṃ niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau||

737.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, dukkhaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā|

sabbasaṅkhārasamathā, sañ‍ñānaṃ uparaudhanā|

aivaṃ dukkhakkhayau hauti, aitaṃ ñatvā yathātathaṃ||

738.

 ‘‘sammaddasā vaidagunau, sammadañ‍ñāya paṇḍitā|

abhibhuyya mārasaṃyaugaṃ, na gacchanti nāgacchanti (sī॰ pī॰) punabbhava’’nti||

(5) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| viñ‍ñāṇassa tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

739.

 ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau||

740.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā|

viñ‍ñāṇūpasamā bhikkhu, nicchātau parinibbutau’’ti||

(6) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ phassapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| phassassa tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca  satthā –

741.

 ‘‘taisaṃ phassaparaitānaṃ, bhavasautānusārinaṃ|

kummaggapaṭipan‍nānaṃ, ārā saṃyaujanakkhayau||

742.

 ‘‘yai ca phassaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, añ‍ñāyupasamai pañ‍ñāya upasamai (syā॰) ratā|

tai vai phassābhisamayā, nicchātā parinibbutā’’ti||

(7) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ vaidanāpac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| vaidanānaṃ tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

743.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ  vā yadi vā dukkhaṃ, adukkhamasukhaṃ saha|

ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca, yaṃ kiñ‍ci atthi vaiditaṃ||

744.

 ‘‘aitaṃ dukkhanti ñatvāna, mausadhammaṃ palaukinaṃ palaukitaṃ (sī॰)|

phussa phussa vayaṃ passaṃ, aivaṃ tattha vijānati viraj‍jati (ka॰ sī॰)|

vaidanānaṃ khayā bhikkhu, nicchātau parinibbutau’’ti||

(8) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| taṇhāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

745.

 ‘‘taṇhādutiyau  purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsaraṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattati||

746.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, taṇhaṃ taṇhā (bahūsu) itivuttakai 15 passitabbaṃ dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

vītataṇhau anādānau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

(9) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ upādānapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| upādānānaṃ upādānassa (syā॰ ka॰) tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

747.

 ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhūtau dukkhaṃ nigacchati|

jātassa maraṇaṃ hauti, aisau dukkhassa sambhavau||

748.

 ‘‘tasmā upādānakkhayā, sammadañ‍ñāya paṇḍitā|

jātikkhayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, na gacchanti punabbhava’’nti||

(10) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ ārambhapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| ārambhānaṃ tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

749.

 ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti, sabbaṃ ārambhapac‍cayā|

ārambhānaṃ niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau||

750.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ  ñatvā, dukkhaṃ ārambhapac‍cayā|

sabbārambhaṃ paṭinissaj‍ja, anārambhai vimuttinau||

751.

 ‘‘ucchin‍nabhavataṇhassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

vikkhīṇau vitiṇṇau (sī॰) jātisaṃsārau, natthi tassa punabbhavau’’ti||

(11) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ āhārapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| āhārānaṃ tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

752.

 ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti, sabbaṃ āhārapac‍cayā|

āhārānaṃ niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau||

753.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, dukkhaṃ āhārapac‍cayā|

sabbāhāraṃ pariñ‍ñāya, sabbāhāramanissitau||

754.

 ‘‘āraugyaṃ  sammadañ‍ñāya, āsavānaṃ parikkhayā|

saṅkhāya saivī dhammaṭṭhau, saṅkhyaṃ saṅkhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) naupaiti vaidagū’’ti||

(12) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti sabbaṃ iñ‍jitapac‍cayāti, ayamaikānupassanā| iñ‍jitānaṃ tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

755.

 ‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhauti, sabbaṃ iñ‍jitapac‍cayā|

iñ‍jitānaṃ niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau||

756.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, dukkhaṃ iñ‍jitapac‍cayā|

tasmā hi aijaṃ vaussaj‍ja, saṅkhārai uparundhiya|

anaijau anupādānau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

(13) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? nissitassa calitaṃ hautīti, ayamaikānupassanā| anissitau na calatīti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

757.

 ‘‘anissitau na calati, nissitau ca upādiyaṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattati||

758.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, nissayaisu mahabbhayaṃ|

anissitau anupādānau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

(14) ‘‘siyā  añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? rūpaihi, bhikkhavai, arūpā āruppā (sī॰ pī॰) santatarāti, ayamaikānupassanā| arūpaihi  niraudhau santatarauti, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

759.

 ‘‘yai ca rūpūpagā sattā, yai ca arūpaṭṭhāyinau āruppavāsinau (sī॰ pī॰)|

niraudhaṃ appajānantā, āgantārau punabbhavaṃ||

760.

 ‘‘yai ca rūpai pariñ‍ñāya, arūpaisu asaṇṭhitā susaṇṭhitā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

niraudhai  yai vimuc‍canti, tai janā mac‍cuhāyinau’’ti||

(15) ‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi…pai॰… kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya idaṃ sac‍canti upanijjhāyitaṃ tadamariyānaṃ aitaṃ musāti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, ayamaikānupassanā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya idaṃ musāti upanijjhāyitaṃ, tadamariyānaṃ aitaṃ sac‍canti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, ayaṃ dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā…pai॰… athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

761.

 ‘‘anattani attamāniṃ attamānī (syā॰), attamānaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), passa laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ|

niviṭṭhaṃ nāmarūpasmiṃ, idaṃ sac‍canti mañ‍ñati||

762.

 ‘‘yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā|

tañhi tassa musā hauti, mausadhammañhi ittaraṃ||

763.

 ‘‘amausadhammaṃ  nibbānaṃ, tadariyā sac‍catau vidū|

tai vai sac‍cābhisamayā, nicchātā parinibbutā’’ti||

(16) ‘‘‘siyā añ‍ñainapi pariyāyaina sammā dvayatānupassanā’ti, iti cai, bhikkhavai, pucchitārau assu; ‘siyā’tissu vacanīyā| kathañ‍ca siyā? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya idaṃ sukhanti upanijjhāyitaṃ, tadamariyānaṃ aitaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, ayamaikānupassanā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya idaṃ dukkhanti upanijjhāyitaṃ tadamariyānaṃ aitaṃ sukhanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, ayaṃ  dutiyānupassanā| aivaṃ sammā dvayatānupassinau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitāti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

764.

 ‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phassā dhammā ca kaivalā|

iṭṭhā kantā manāpā ca, yāvatatthīti vuc‍cati||

765.

 ‘‘sadaivakassa laukassa, aitai vau sukhasammatā|

yattha caitai nirujjhanti, taṃ naisaṃ dukkhasammataṃ||

766.

 ‘‘sukhanti  diṭṭhamariyaihi, sak‍kāyassuparaudhanaṃ|

pac‍canīkamidaṃ hauti, sabbalaukaina passataṃ||

767.

 ‘‘yaṃ  parai sukhatau āhu, tadariyā āhu dukkhatau|

yaṃ parai dukkhatau āhu, tadariyā sukhatau vidū||

768.

 ‘‘passa dhammaṃ durājānaṃ, sampamūḷhaitthaviddasu sampamūḷhaittha aviddasu (sī॰ pī॰), sammūḷhaittha aviddasu (?)|

nivutānaṃ tamau hauti, andhakārau apassataṃ||

769.

 ‘‘satañ‍ca vivaṭaṃ hauti, ālaukau passatāmiva|

santikai na vijānanti, maggā dhammassa kauvidā||

770.

 ‘‘bhavarāgaparaitaihi, bhavasautānusāribhi|

māradhaiyyānupan‍naihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

771.

 ‘‘kau nu añ‍ñatramariyaihi, padaṃ sambuddhumarahati|

yaṃ padaṃ sammadañ‍ñāya, parinibbanti anāsavā’’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiṃ ca imasmiṃ khau (sī॰) pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti|

dvayatānupassanāsuttaṃ dvādasamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ  –

sac‍caṃ  upadhi avij‍jā ca, saṅkhārai viñ‍ñāṇapañ‍camaṃ|

phassavaidaniyā taṇhā, upādānārambhaāhārā|

iñ‍jitaṃ calitaṃ rūpaṃ, sac‍caṃ dukkhaina sauḷasāti||

mahāvaggau tatiyau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

pabbaj‍jā ca padhānañ‍ca, subhāsitañ‍ca sundari|

māghasuttaṃ sabhiyau ca, sailau sal‍lañ‍ca vuc‍cati||

vāsaiṭṭhau cāpi kaukāli, nālakau dvayatānupassanā|

dvādasaitāni suttāni, mahāvaggauti vuc‍catīti||


4. aṭṭhakavaggau

1. kāmasuttaṃ

772.

 kāmaṃ  kāmayamānassa, tassa cai taṃ samijjhati|

addhā pītimanau hauti, laddhā mac‍cau yadicchati||

773.

 tassa cai kāmayānassa kāmayamānassa (ka॰), chandajātassa jantunau|

tai kāmā parihāyanti, sal‍laviddhauva ruppati||

774.

 yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padā sirau|

saumaṃ sau imaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattati||

775.

 khaittaṃ vatthuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ vā, gavassaṃ gavāssaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dāsapaurisaṃ|

thiyau bandhū puthu kāmai, yau narau anugijjhati||

776.

 abalā naṃ balīyanti, maddantainaṃ parissayā|

tatau naṃ dukkhamanvaiti, nāvaṃ bhin‍namivaudakaṃ||

777.

 tasmā jantu sadā satau, kāmāni parivaj‍jayai|

tai pahāya tarai aughaṃ, nāvaṃ sitvāva siñ‍citvā (sī॰) pāragūti||

kāmasuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. guhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ

778.

 sattau  guhāyaṃ bahunābhichan‍nau, tiṭṭhaṃ narau mauhanasmiṃ pagāḷhau|

dūrai  vivaikā hi tathāvidhau sau, kāmā hi laukai na hi suppahāyā||

779.

 icchānidānā  bhavasātabaddhā, tai duppamuñ‍cā na hi añ‍ñamaukkhā|

pacchā purai vāpi apaikkhamānā, imaiva kāmai purimaiva jappaṃ||

780.

 kāmaisu giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā, avadāniyā tai visamai niviṭṭhā|

dukkhūpanītā paridaivayanti, kiṃsū bhavissāma itau cutāsai||

781.

 tasmā hi sikkhaitha idhaiva jantu, yaṃ kiñ‍ci jañ‍ñā visamanti laukai|

na tassa haitū visamaṃ caraiyya, appañhidaṃ jīvitamāhu dhīrā||

782.

 passāmi laukai pariphandamānaṃ, pajaṃ imaṃ taṇhagataṃ bhavaisu|

hīnā  narā mac‍cumukhai lapanti, avītataṇhāsai bhavābhavaisu||

783.

 mamāyitai passatha phandamānai, macchaiva appaudakai khīṇasautai|

aitampi disvā amamau caraiyya, bhavaisu āsattimakubbamānau||

784.

 ubhausu antaisu vinaiyya chandaṃ, phassaṃ pariñ‍ñāya anānugiddhau|

yadattagarahī tadakubbamānau, na lippatī na limpatī (syā॰ ka॰) diṭṭhasutaisu dhīrau||

785.

 sañ‍ñaṃ  pariñ‍ñā vitaraiyya aughaṃ, pariggahaisu muni naupalittau|

abbūḷhasal‍lau caramappamattau, nāsīsatī nāsiṃsatī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) laukamimaṃ parañ‍cāti||

guhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ dutiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. duṭṭhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ

786.

 vadanti  vai duṭṭhamanāpi aikai, athaupi vai sac‍camanā vadanti|

vādañ‍ca jātaṃ muni nau upaiti, tasmā munī natthi khilau kuhiñ‍ci||

787.

 sakañhi diṭṭhiṃ kathamac‍cayaiyya, chandānunītau ruciyā niviṭṭhau|

sayaṃ samattāni pakubbamānau, yathā hi jānaiyya tathā vadaiyya||

788.

 yau attanau sīlavatāni jantu, anānupuṭṭhauva paraisa parassa (ka॰) pāva pāvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

anariyadhammaṃ kusalā tamāhu, yau ātumānaṃ sayamaiva pāva||

789.

 santau ca bhikkhu abhinibbutattau, itihanti sīlaisu akatthamānau|

tamariyadhammaṃ kusalā vadanti, yassussadā natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai||

790.

 pakappitā  saṅkhatā yassa dhammā, purakkhatā puraikkhatā (sī॰) santi avīvadātā|

yadattani  passati ānisaṃsaṃ, taṃ nissitau kuppapaṭic‍ca santiṃ||

791.

 diṭṭhīnivaisā na hi svātivattā, dhammaisu nicchaiyya samuggahītaṃ|

tasmā narau taisu nivaisanaisu, nirassatī ādiyatī ca dhammaṃ||

792.

 dhaunassa  hi natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai, pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavaisu|

māyañ‍ca mānañ‍ca pahāya dhaunau, sa kaina gacchaiyya anūpayau sau||

793.

 upayau hi dhammaisu upaiti vādaṃ, anūpayaṃ kaina kathaṃ vadaiyya|

attā nirattā attaṃ nirattaṃ (bahūsu) na hi tassa atthi, adhausi sau diṭṭhimidhaiva sabbanti||

duṭṭhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ tatiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. suddhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ

794.

 passāmi  suddhaṃ paramaṃ araugaṃ, diṭṭhaina saṃsuddhi narassa hauti|

aivābhijānaṃ  aitābhijānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) paramanti ñatvā, suddhānupassīti pac‍caiti ñāṇaṃ||

795.

 diṭṭhaina  cai suddhi narassa hauti, ñāṇaina vā sau pajahāti dukkhaṃ|

añ‍ñaina sau sujjhati saupadhīkau, diṭṭhī hi naṃ pāva tathā vadānaṃ||

796.

 na brāhmaṇau añ‍ñatau suddhimāha, diṭṭhai sutai sīlavatai mutai vā|

puñ‍ñai ca pāpai ca anūpalittau, attañ‍jahau nayidha pakubbamānau||

797.

 purimaṃ pahāya aparaṃ sitāsai, aijānugā tai na taranti saṅgaṃ|

tai uggahāyanti nirassajanti, kapīva sākhaṃ pamuñ‍caṃ gahāyaṃ pamukhaṃ gahāya (syā॰), pamuñ‍ca gahāya (ka॰)||

798.

 sayaṃ  samādāya vatāni jantu, uc‍cāvacaṃ gacchati sañ‍ñasattau|

vidvā ca vaidaihi samaic‍ca dhammaṃ, na uc‍cāvacaṃ gacchati bhūripañ‍ñau||

799.

 sa sabbadhammaisu visainibhūtau, yaṃ kiñ‍ci diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā|

tamaiva  dassiṃ vivaṭaṃ carantaṃ, kainīdha laukasmi vikappayaiyya||

800.

 na kappayanti na puraikkharaunti, ac‍cantasuddhīti na tai vadanti|

ādānaganthaṃ  gathitaṃ visaj‍ja, āsaṃ na kubbanti kuhiñ‍ci laukai||

801.

 sīmātigau brāhmaṇau tassa natthi, ñatvā va disvā va ñatvā ca disvā ca (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) samuggahītaṃ|

na rāgarāgī na virāgarattau, tassīdha natthī paramuggahītanti||

suddhaṭṭhakasuttaṃ catutthaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. paramaṭṭhakasuttaṃ

802.

 paramanti  diṭṭhīsu paribbasānau, yaduttari kurutai jantu laukai|

hīnāti añ‍ñai tatau sabbamāha, tasmā vivādāni avītivattau||

803.

 yadattanī passati ānisaṃsaṃ, diṭṭhai  sutai sīlavatai sīlabbatai (syā॰) mutai vā|

tadaiva sau tattha samuggahāya, nihīnatau passati sabbamañ‍ñaṃ||

804.

 taṃ  vāpi ganthaṃ kusalā vadanti, yaṃ nissitau passati hīnamañ‍ñaṃ|

tasmā hi diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā, sīlabbataṃ bhikkhu na nissayaiyya||

805.

 diṭṭhimpi  laukasmiṃ na kappayaiyya, ñāṇaina vā sīlavataina vāpi|

samauti attānamanūpanaiyya, hīnau na mañ‍ñaitha visaisi vāpi||

806.

 attaṃ pahāya anupādiyānau, ñāṇaipi sau nissayaṃ nau karauti|

sa vai viyattaisu viyuttaisu (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), dviyattaisu (ka॰) na vaggasārī, diṭṭhimpi diṭṭhimapi (ka॰) sau na pac‍caiti kiñ‍ci||

807.

 yassūbhayantai paṇidhīdha natthi, bhavābhavāya idha vā huraṃ vā|

nivaisanā tassa na santi kaici, dhammaisu nicchaiyya samuggahītaṃ||

808.

 tassīdha diṭṭhai va sutai mutai vā, pakappitā  natthi aṇūpi sañ‍ñā|

taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ diṭṭhimanādiyānaṃ, kainīdha laukasmiṃ vikappayaiyya||

809.

 na kappayanti na puraikkharaunti, dhammāpi taisaṃ na paṭicchitāsai|

na  brāhmaṇau sīlavataina naiyyau, pāraṅgatau na pac‍caiti tādīti||

paramaṭṭhakasuttaṃ pañ‍camaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. jarāsuttaṃ

810.

 appaṃ  vata jīvitaṃ idaṃ, auraṃ vassasatāpi miyyati mīyati (sī॰ aṭṭha॰)|

yau caipi atic‍ca jīvati, atha khau sau jarasāpi miyyati||

811.

 saucanti janā mamāyitai, na hi santi na hi santā (sī॰), na hī santi (katthaci) nic‍cā pariggahā|

vinābhāvasantamaividaṃ, iti disvā nāgāramāvasai||

812.

 maraṇainapi taṃ pahīyati pahiyyati (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), yaṃ purisau mamidanti mamayidanti (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), mamāyanti (ka॰) mañ‍ñati|

aitampi viditvā aitaṃ disvāna (niddaisai), aitampi viditva (?) paṇḍitau, na mamattāya namaitha māmakau||

813.

 supinaina yathāpi saṅgataṃ, paṭibuddhau purisau na passati|

aivampi  piyāyitaṃ janaṃ, paitaṃ kālakataṃ na passati||

814.

 diṭṭhāpi  sutāpi tai janā, yaisaṃ nāmamidaṃ pavuc‍cati nāmamaivā vasissati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

nāmaṃyaivāvasissati, akkhaiyyaṃ paitassa jantunau||

815.

 saukapparidaivamaccharaṃ saukaparidaivamaccharaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), saukaṃ paridaivamaccharaṃ (?), na jahanti giddhā mamāyitai|

tasmā munayau pariggahaṃ, hitvā acariṃsu khaimadassinau||

816.

 patilīnacarassa  bhikkhunau, bhajamānassa vivittamāsanaṃ|

sāmaggiyamāhu tassa taṃ, yau attānaṃ bhavanai na dassayai||

817.

 sabbattha munī anissitau, na piyaṃ kubbati naupi appiyaṃ|

tasmiṃ paridaivamaccharaṃ, paṇṇai vāri yathā na limpati lippati (sī॰ pī॰)||

818.

 udabindu yathāpi paukkharai, padumai vāri yathā na limpati|

aivaṃ muni naupalimpati, yadidaṃ diṭṭhasutaṃ mutaisu vā||

819.

 dhaunau  na hi taina mañ‍ñati, yadidaṃ diṭṭhasutaṃ mutaisu vā|

nāñ‍ñaina visuddhimicchati, na hi sau raj‍jati nau viraj‍jatīti||

jarāsuttaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. tissamaittaiyyasuttaṃ

820.

 ‘‘maithunamanuyuttassa, (ic‍cāyasmā tissau maittaiyyau) vighātaṃ brūhi mārisa|

sutvāna tava sāsanaṃ, vivaikai sikkhissāmasai||

821.

 ‘‘maithunamanuyuttassa, (maittaiyyāti bhagavā) mussatai vāpi sāsanaṃ|

micchā  ca paṭipaj‍jati, aitaṃ tasmiṃ anāriyaṃ||

822.

 ‘‘aikau  pubbai caritvāna, maithunaṃ yau nisaivati|

yānaṃ bhantaṃ va taṃ laukai, hīnamāhu puthuj‍janaṃ||

823.

 ‘‘yasau kitti ca yā pubbai, hāyatai vāpi tassa sā|

aitampi disvā sikkhaitha, maithunaṃ vippahātavai||

824.

 ‘‘saṅkappaihi paraitau sau, kapaṇau viya jhāyati|

sutvā paraisaṃ nigghausaṃ, maṅku hauti tathāvidhau||

825.

 ‘‘atha satthāni kurutai, paravādaihi cauditau|

aisa khvassa mahāgaidhau, mausavaj‍jaṃ pagāhati||

826.

 ‘‘paṇḍitauti samañ‍ñātau, aikacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhitau|

athāpi sa cāpi (niddaisai) maithunai yuttau, mandauva parikissati parikilissati (sī॰)||

827.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ  ñatvā, muni pubbāparai idha|

aikacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kayirā, na nisaivaitha maithunaṃ||

828.

 ‘‘vivaikañ‍ñaiva sikkhaitha, aitadariyānamuttamaṃ|

na taina saiṭṭhau mañ‍ñaitha, sa vai nibbānasantikai||

829.

 ‘‘rittassa muninau caratau, kāmaisu anapaikkhinau|

aughatiṇṇassa pihayanti, kāmaisu gadhitā gathitā (sī॰) pajā’’ti||

tissamaittaiyyasuttaṃ sattamaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. pasūrasuttaṃ

830.

 idhaiva  suddhi iti vādayanti vidiyanti (sī॰ pī॰), nāñ‍ñaisu dhammaisu visuddhimāhu|

yaṃ nissitā tattha subhaṃ vadānā, pac‍caikasac‍caisu puthū niviṭṭhā||

831.
